  - *Constraints:* A non-zero refusing count means STOP and report; the controller decides each row (re-key, detach or purge), never an automatic repair.
- **1, body helpers, guard, rekey, id guard (after the NAS ALTER TYPE migration and the repairs A–C and personal-space migrations; before the moves migration)**: The rabbit_private schema; the preflight; the SB-2 indexes; storage_object_claimed, storage_object_current_project, file_key_admissible, files_body_follow, files_body_retire, s3_body_moves_on (false), project_id_tied_elsewhere; fn_files_body_guard and its trigger; rekey_file_body; fn_projects_id_reuse_guard and its trigger; REVOKE and GRANT. Post-conditions on comment-stripped text, plus owner rolbypassrls.
  - *Constraints:* One transaction, no enum use (provider compared as ::text). Rolled-back rehearsal of the migration plus the new suite and every re-pointed probe first; collected = planned. Ship the adapter changes in the same release. Old builds fail with a named error: refile puts the object back; moveReceipt says its sentence.
- **2, storage policies, signer predicate, meter**: DROP + CREATE of the 8 base policies; can_presign_object; workspace_petal_committed_bytes. Post-conditions as in schema_changes, including the md5 pins.
  - *Constraints:* After step 1. EXPLAIN ANALYZE of a 100-object list and a createSignedUrl on dev, before and after, recorded in the hand-off.
- **3, moves (moves area), the shared vocabulary, capture and realtime**: moth_move_items with p_rehome; fn_moth_apply_moves calling follow and retire; file_events CHECK union; capture with 'refiled' and no 'moved' on a project change; broadcast split.
  - *Constraints:* Post-conditions assert follow and retire are called, the broadcast split and the history contract are present, and wilson.checked_move is named by the guard. s3 cross-project moves are still refused (switch off).
- **4, Edge Functions and client release**: storage-presign, _shared/readUrls.ts plus storage-read-urls, storage-gc, teardownCollect via operator-workspaces; deno KNOWN re-measured.
  - *Constraints:* Deployed on each environment AFTER steps 1–3 are there. Live state read from functions list plus a source comparison, not from CLI success. Rollback in reverse, except storage-presign once step 5 is on.
- **5, s3 moves on (per environment)**: s3_body_moves_on() restated as true.
  - *Constraints:* Only after step 4's storage-presign is verified on that environment; runbook check recorded.
- **6, Move to UI (Moth and the Files page)**: Ships with copyBodyForMove and the needs_copy retry.
  - *Constraints:* After step 4 on that environment (until then moved s3 copies cannot be previewed in the destination, and teardown would strand a moved copy from a purged origin).

#### Tests

- **supabase/tests/rls/NNN_stable_file_bodies.sql (new cross-table suite, next free number at build, added by hand to the replay list)**: Objects seeded as postgres with owner_id. POSITIVE: F made in shared A and moved by association into B: a B-only reader reads F and its .jpg; after hard-deleting A, B still reads it and teardown/meter count it; a B writer can regenerate the picture; row-less objects (PROJECT.json, RATES.json for a money-cleared reader, a bin poster, connection-check) read as before; a bin poster replaced then restored to the older key still reads (SFB-02); an orphan-swept deterministic key can be carried again after the drain (SFB-01). NEGATIVE: an A-only reader gets 0 for moved F; another workspace's member gets 0, including through a W2 project re-taking A's purged id (SEC-1); a non-money member gets 0 for an is_financial row under an ordinary key; a trashed F gets 0 for everyone; with A still live, purge F as postgres, then an A-only reader gets 0 with the queue row, and 0 with the queue row removed (arm 3); delete_own's qual names NOT claimed and, evaluated as the uploader, is false for a claimed key (SFB-06); an INSERT at a purged moved key is refused; a direct RPC call of rabbit_private helpers is impossible (anon/authenticated have no EXECUTE on follow and retire); a planted definer called as authenticated that UPDATEs files.project_id without the marker is refused. Text probe: the functions naming 'wilson.checked_move' are exactly {fn_moth_apply_moves, fn_files_body_guard, fn_files_move_guard}, those naming 'wilson.body_rekey' {rekey_file_body, fn_files_body_guard}, those naming 'wilson.network_share_writer' {network_share_catalogue_apply, fn_files_network_share_guard, fn_files_body_guard, fn_realtime_broadcast}. PLANTED CONTROLS (each restored by sha1): restore 0042's second-segment hop and the A-only probe goes red; re-scope claimed to the caller's workspace and the W2 probe goes red; drop arm 3 and the arm-3 probe goes red; make current_project DEFINER and the trashed and is_financial probes go red; drop NOT claimed from delete_own and the qual probe goes red; drop file_id IS NOT NULL from arm 2 and the poster probe goes red.
- **supabase/tests/rls/05_files.sql (extend, re-point existing UPDATE probes)**: A client UPDATE of each of the seven columns gives 42501; resending the same values plus a description change passes. INSERT refusals, one probe each: key under another project; bin_files, connection-check or FINANCE third segment; a 3-segment key; a key held by another row; a pending queue key; a purged moved key; supabase with no object; picture ≠ path.jpg; google_drive or network_share provider; another project's folder; a non-money row in a locked folder; a money key by a non-money member (same sentence whether or not the object exists, SEC-7). Valid key plus object: accepted; same id and path again (insertOrSkip): no error. rekey_file_body refusals: across the second segment, across LEGAL, out of INVOICES, into INVOICES without money access, s3, a moved row, a trashed row, a trashed project, a non-owner on a personal-space file; a stale from-path gives false; a valid rekey gives true and one 'moved' event. PLANTED: drop the trigger, make admissible return true, drop the second-segment arm, drop the id exclusion, drop the deleted_at arm: each goes red.
- **supabase/tests/rls/61_workspace_storage_s3.sql (extend)**: can_presign_object: get/head on a live s3 copy and its .jpg is true; trashed false; purged moved false; moved row true for destination readers and false for source-only readers; put/del over any claimed key false; connection-check key gives the legacy answer; fresh unclaimed key gives the legacy write answer. SEC-2: inserting an s3 row at left(K,-4) with picture K, or at K||'.jpg', is refused (unique violation surfaced as the guard's 42501). PLANTED: drop files_s3_namespace_uidx and the collision probes go red; route claimed get through the prefix and the source-only probe goes red.
- **Moves suite (moves area) plus 33_file_lifecycle and 78_file_events_money (extend)**: Body-follow: body_missing, body_shape (legacy bin_files row), s3_moves_off, body_elsewhere, needs_copy for a local_server row and for a key naming a personal space or private project, copy_not_needed, copy_refused (inadmissible to_path, wrong provider, missing object). A re-homed move: one UPDATE, new key under the destination, the old copy and picture queued (reason file-purged, file_id, kind), no B-readable row or event names the personal space's id, no 'moved' event, exactly two 'refiled' events with object details. Undo of a re-homed move works by association; an undo that would need a copy is refused. Association move: no queue row, no 'purged' event; a legacy picture is detached and queued. A personal-space upload row is never local_server. PLANTED: remove the personal arm of the re-home rule and the id-leak probe goes red; let 'moved' fire on a project change and the event-count probe goes red.
- **supabase/tests/rls 63, 66 (meter), 79, 80, 81, 83, 87, 90, 95 (re-point and extend), 35_platform_audit (rabbit_private)**: The 8 rabbit_files% count and the money text pins hold. Meter: copies moved out of A and A purged are still counted; a foreign workspace's are never counted; an unmoved copy is counted once; planted: drop the arm, then drop its NOT EXISTS, each goes red. 79/80/81: client inserts re-keyed to admissible keys with seeded objects, CHECK-code probes kept as postgres twins. 35: no rabbit_private definer is executable by anon, all have search_path set.
- **vitest: storagePresignBoundary / readUrls; teardownCollect; storageGcShotTwins; supabaseAdapter (updateFile, rewriteFileRow, copyBodyForMove); runMigration (moveReceipt, refused-upload probe, locked-folder filing); config.toml pin; reservedObjects lockstep with the SQL row-less shapes; segment allowlist lockstep (uploadContainerFor, cloudObjectPathFor 'files', INVOICES/LEGAL builders, copyBodyForMove 'files')**: classifyOwned signs a moved row's key and refuses a key no visible row owns; no canReadProject call; storage-presign calls can_presign_object per op. Teardown collects a moved copy tied by file_events and rejects another workspace's. rowNamesTwin carries the provider filter and no project filter. updateFile never sends the seven columns. rewriteFileRow uses the RPC and puts the object back on false. copyBodyForMove builds an admissible key and refuses off the holding desktop. The source scan fails on any .from('files').update( whose literal names a guarded column. Each is red first on the pre-change source, then green. Full run with --maxWorkers=4 and no .env.local.

#### Risks

- Per-object policy cost: every listed, signed or read object now runs a definer claimed check (up to three index probes) and, when claimed, an invoker files lookup.
  - *Mitigation:* Every lookup is indexed (SB-2); the IFs stop at the first hit; the EXPLAIN ANALYZE before and after is recorded in the hand-off. F7's policy wave may wrap the constant parts later.
- Re-homing copies bytes. Large videos leaving a personal space or a private project's computer take time, and on Petal the copy counts toward quota until the old copy is drained.
  - *Mitigation:* Only those moves copy; all others are association-only. The move shows progress and says plainly when the plan limit refuses the copy (INFERRED whether storage copy passes through reserve_upload_bytes; the build measures it). The old copy is queued at commit.
- A purged moved copy falls back to its origin's readers if both independent writers (queue enqueue and purge certificate) swallow an error on the same purge. The window lasts until the next orphan scan removes the object.
  - *Mitigation:* Separate arms pinned separately; the residual is recorded in OUTSTANDING.
- Within-project adoption: a writer of P can claim another member's unclaimed in-flight upload under P, including as a re-home target.
  - *Mitigation:* No confidentiality gain, since P's writers can already read unclaimed P keys. The uploader retries. INFORMATIONAL beside OSP-07.
- Keys stop naming the current project. A shared origin's id and original name stay visible to the destination, and an origin trashed later still shows its id.
  - *Mitigation:* Only shared origins keep their key (SB-12); handbook rule: 'a key's prefix is where a file was created; group by files.project_id'.
- Guard and the existing probes: 05, 33, 78, 79, 80, 81, 83, 90 and 95 contain client writes the guard now refuses. Old clients during the deploy window (refile, moveReceipt).
  - *Mitigation:* Re-point in the same commit, after measuring every suite that writes public.files; old-client failures are graceful and named (S4c-14 class).
- Signed URLs minted before a move or trash stay valid until expiry (thumbnails 1 h, s3 stream 3600 s, read-once 300 s); desktop thumbnail caches survive a move.
  - *Mitigation:* Stated in the walkthrough and handbook; live channels are Session 4's audience work.
- session_user-based exemption: a future non-API maintenance role, or a pooler that changes session_user, would alter which writes need the marker.
  - *Mitigation:* Exemption is a positive list (postgres, supabase_admin), so anything else must carry the marker (fails closed). Probe as authenticated through a definer pins it; runbook notes the list.
- Storage-presign rolled back after s3 moves are on would sign moved copies for the origin.
  - *Mitigation:* SB-13 switch, applied only after verification; runbook check query (any s3 row whose prefix ≠ project_id) blocks a rollback.
- Cross-area drift: the moves area still names a provider list, MV-14 refuses moves between local projects, and the NAS area does association-only moves.
  - *Mitigation:* The interfaces below are the reconciliation list; the integrator settles names, GUCs and the event vocabulary before build.

#### Interfaces with the other areas

- **files column guard: fn_files_body_guard / trg_files_body_guard** (provides): BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.files. Client roles: refuse changes to {project_id, workspace_id, folder_id, storage_provider, storage_path, thumbnail_url, is_financial} (42501). INSERT needs provider ∈ {supabase, s3, local_server}, file_key_admissible and a derived picture. API-session server roles (session_user ∉ {postgres, supabase_admin}): workspace_id never; project_id or folder_id≠NULL needs wilson.checked_move='on'; locator or is_financial needs checked_move='on', wilson.body_rekey='on' (provider unchanged), or wilson.network_share_writer='on' (network_share rows). folder_id→NULL always passes (RI).
- **trusted writers of guarded columns** (provides): fn_moth_apply_moves (sets wilson.checked_move='on' via set_config(…, true) and clears it before returning; writes project_id, folder_id, links, and follow's set); rekey_file_body (wilson.body_rekey); network_share_catalogue_apply (wilson.network_share_writer). No other function may name these literals (pgTAP text probe).
- **move operation and body hook** (needs): public.moth_move_items(p_file_ids uuid[], p_folder_ids uuid[], p_dest_project uuid, p_dest_folder uuid, p_rehome jsonb DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb) RETURNS jsonb. For each cross-project file it calls rabbit_private.files_body_follow(p_file uuid, p_dest_project uuid, p_rehome jsonb) RETURNS jsonb {set:{storage_provider?,storage_path?,thumbnail_url?}, retire:[{bucket,path,provider,kind}]}, after the Moth exclusion and write checks, before its single UPDATE. Then rabbit_private.files_body_retire(p_file uuid, p_retire jsonb) after the UPDATE. Refusal codes: body_elsewhere, s3_moves_off, body_missing, body_shape, needs_copy, copy_not_needed, copy_refused. p_rehome item: {file_id uuid, to_provider 'supabase'|'s3', to_path text, to_thumb text|null}. moth_undo_move replays association only and refuses when follow answers needs_copy.
- **body provider values** (provides): public.storage_provider = supabase | google_drive | local_server | s3, plus network_share (NAS area, own ALTER TYPE migration). Client INSERT: supabase, s3, local_server. Association-movable: supabase; s3 only while rabbit_private.s3_body_moves_on(). Re-home required: local_server (copy-up from the holding desktop), or a key whose second-segment project exists, is_private and ≠ destination. Refused: google_drive. network_share: only by the NAS writer (real-file move recommended, SB-22). Company target for a re-home: the active provider (mode 'byos' only, otherwise petal) petal→supabase, s3→s3, network→NAS writer (needs).
- **rekey_file_body** (provides): public.rekey_file_body(p_file uuid, p_from_path text, p_to_path text, p_from_thumb text, p_to_thumb text, p_financial boolean DEFAULT false) RETURNS boolean (false = compare-and-set miss; 42501 = not found or not writable). Same project prefix only, never s3, never a moved row. Session 4 adds the sticky lease arm.
- **file_events vocabulary and capture** (needs): One CHECK (DROP + ADD, full list restated): uploaded, downloaded, moved, relinked, trashed, restored, purged, upload_abandoned, viewed_remote, refiled, catalogued. The capture on a project_id change writes 'refiled' under OLD.project_id (old_path, details {direction:'out', folder_id}) and under NEW.project_id (new_path, details {direction:'in', folder_id}); details is never NULL; 'moved' is not fired when project_id changes. 'purged' keeps 0074's shape (project_id = OLD.project_id, old_path = OLD.storage_path), which arm 3 reads.
- **realtime split for files** (needs): fn_realtime_broadcast files arm (restated once from 0098): money RETURN NULL first; on OLD.project_id ≠ NEW.project_id, broadcast_changes('rabbit:project:'||OLD.project_id,'DELETE',…,NULL,OLD) and broadcast_changes('rabbit:project:'||NEW.project_id,'INSERT',…,NEW,NULL), then RETURN NULL.
- **personal-space classifier** (needs): projects.personal_owner_id with projects_personal_shape_chk (personal ⇒ is_private = true AND created_by = personal_owner_id). Body rules read projects.is_private, so personal spaces count as private. projectIsPrivate must not route a personal row to local_server (probe: a personal upload row is never local_server).
- **workspace-channel suppression** (needs): fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast (repairs C) never carries a private or personal projects row, so a project id in a shared-origin key cannot be resolved to a hidden title. files rows are not on the workspace channel.
- **history capture** (needs): moth_history stores no storage_path, thumbnail_url or locator (tables area allowlist CHECK). edit_history: a files diff whose before and after project_id differ is readable only by a reader passing both sides' audience, or is stored with project_id, folder_id and storage_path stripped (repairs B).
- **projects id-reuse guard** (provides): trg_projects_id_reuse_guard BEFORE INSERT ON public.projects (client roles): refuses NEW.id with no projects row that rabbit_private.project_id_tied_elsewhere(NEW.id, NEW.workspace_id) ties to another workspace. It must sort after trg_projects_populate_workspace, or use current_workspace_id(). May be folded into the repairs or personal projects guard.
- **storage authority helpers** (provides): rabbit_private.storage_object_claimed(p_bucket text, p_name text) RETURNS boolean; rabbit_private.storage_object_current_project(p_bucket text, p_name text) RETURNS uuid (INVOKER); rabbit_private.file_key_admissible(p_provider text, p_path text, p_project uuid, p_file uuid, p_folder uuid, p_financial boolean) RETURNS boolean; public.can_presign_object(p_path text, p_op text) RETURNS boolean.
- **desktop-only and office-server bodies** (needs): Moves area's Local Server route: a cross-project move moves the real file (same storage_path string, verified size, durable pending-move record), per her answer 2. NAS area: a real-file move between project folders and the bin-folder delete (answer 5) under wilson.network_share_writer; the browser says to use the desktop app; a re-home target for network companies.

### 2.2 The personal space and who sees what

Findings: 2 partly, 23 taken.

**Scope as resolved:** Personal space and who sees what (projects-row personal space, owner-only writes, Files/Moth audiences, Moth read path, desktop and office-server personal spaces)

**Summary:** REVISED design (replaces the draft). Each person's personal space is an ordinary projects row: personal_owner_id uuid (no FK), with CHECK personal_owner_id IS NULL OR (is_private AND created_by = personal_owner_id), and a partial unique index on (workspace_id, personal_owner_id). Being is_private, it inherits projects_select (0072), passes_project_privacy (0082), the 0014 live-parent hops and the repaired workspace broadcast. So reads are owner plus workspace admins, and no read policy is restated. Three changes from the draft, all settled from the code.
(1) Writes are owner-only at the database, not just in the interface (SEC-6). The latest bodies of can_write_project, can_comment_project and can_manage_project_roster (0013) and can_edit_shot_lists (0084) are restated with one personal arm in front: on a personal project the answer is personal_owner_id = auth.uid(), and false for roster changes. That one change covers every write policy, fn_trash_authz, can_presign_project_write and the NAS catalogue RPC. Admins reach the space from Files read-only, and projects_delete gains AND personal_owner_id IS NULL.
(2) This area no longer owns the created_by pin, the is_private-flip rule, the workspace-broadcast skip, the project_members_select hop or the edit_history arm. These are hard prerequisites owned by the privacy-repairs area (D4, D6, D7, D11). The draft's broadcast arm failed open on cascades (SEC-2/PSA-09). Its edit_history arm called a function clients cannot execute (PSA-01) and covered files only (PSA-02).
(3) Her answers decide storage:
- Petal and s3 companies upload personal files into the company's storage. projectIsPrivate stops treating a personal row as 'this computer', and a new files guard refuses local_server and google_drive bodies in a personal project.
- Office-server companies (answer 3) keep them in a Personal_<owner hex> folder on the office server. They are added from the desktop, which copies the file there and refreshes the NAS catalogue, and the add dialog tells people in plain words that anyone who opens the server directly can find them.
- The signed-out desktop gets one personal space per computer and OS user, under userData and outside rabbit-data. It is safe from shared or copied data folders, the migration and archive-and-clear (SEC-7, PSA-05, PSA-06).
Answer 4: an admin opening another person's space in Files writes a server-stamped app_events 'admin' line (WIL-4114), which other admins see in Logs.
Workspace Takeout excludes personal spaces and their children (SEC-4). Team Members drops null and personal seat rows (SEC-1). The Moth read path keeps INVOKER RPCs over RLS narrowed by one DEFINER helper. That helper now scopes the receipt arm to the file's own project (SEC-3), checks the whole folder chain (SEC-10), clears foreign folder ids in the positive form (SEC-9), and moth_islands returns a server-computed is_mine (PSA-14).
The client predicate is no longer a new action. canOnProject gains isPersonal and isPersonalOwner, mirroring the restated SQL helpers, and one MATRIX table is read by pgTAP and vitest.
Answer 6: moth_prefs (last view, first visit, recent folders) moves to Session 2 with its spec recorded. Session 1 builds the personal space, the read path, the admin record and the guards only.

#### Findings and their dispositions

- **SEC-1 [blocker] — taken.** Hard prerequisite on the repairs area's D11: project_members_select restated from 0020 plus the projects hop. TeamMembersPage drops rows whose embed is null or carries personal_owner_id (probe through personalSpacesAvailable). Admins get personal spaces only from listPersonalSpaces in Files. Suite 18 probes: a member reads 0 seat rows of a colleague's personal space, an admin reads them. Mounted Team test: no 'Untitled' and no 'Personal space'.
  - *Evidence:* 0020_admin_grants_and_alignment.sql L254-259: project_members_select = workspace + has_active_membership only (latest; 0013 L167 'Rosters are workspace-visible'). TeamMembersPage.jsx L228-239 selects projects(title, deleted_at), skips only deleted_at, pushes row.projects?.title || 'Untitled'. fn_projects_auto_staff (0020 L386-426) seats the creator, so ensure_personal_space creates a seat row.
- **SEC-2 [blocker] — taken.** The draft's broadcast arm is withdrawn. The broadcast is owned by repairs D7, which judges each side fail-closed in the 0101 shape and sends AUDIENCE with no row. This area adds only the client rule (no merge of is_private or personal rows) and a suite 23 cascade probe requirement: hard-deleting a private project that holds an assigned task broadcasts nothing.
  - *Evidence:* 0101 fn_task_items_ws_broadcast L185-192 is fail-closed: IF NOT EXISTS (projects p WHERE p.id=v_project AND NOT p.is_private) RETURN NULL, with the comment 'A project gone (a cascade) is skipped the same way'. The draft's arm (EXISTS ... p.is_private) is false once a cascade has removed the parent. 0018 L102-137 sends NEW/OLD whole.
- **SEC-3 [major] — taken.** The receipt arm becomes NOT EXISTS (expenses e WHERE e.project_id = f.project_id AND e.workspace_id = f.workspace_id AND e.file_ids @> ARRAY[f.id]). The GIN index stays. Probe: an expense in project B naming a file of project A does not hide it. Planted: drop the project equality and the probe goes red. The same-project oracle goes to OUTSTANDING.
  - *Evidence:* 0037: expenses.file_ids UUID[] (L299, no FK); expenses_insert WITH CHECK = workspace + can_access_project_money(project_id) (L493-498); can_access_project_money admits project_role_for='manager' (L99-112). 0076 L193-196 says a client can point an expense at any file. The owner's auto-staffed manager seat gives the money gate on their own space.
- **SEC-4 [major] — taken.** Takeout computes the personal set P first (projects where personal_owner_id is not null, behind the probe). It skips projects rows in P and keeps a dropped-id set: a row is dropped when its project_id is in P, or when any of entity_id, asset_id, task_id, phase_id, file_id, predecessor_id, successor_id or run_id is a dropped id. Tables are already parents first. The manifest's excluded list names 'personal spaces (owner-only; admins reach them from Files)'. Mounted test with a planted control.
  - *Evidence:* WorkspaceTakeout.jsx TAKEOUT_TABLES L35-58 includes projects, project_members, files, file_events, comments, edit_history; fetchAll L79-107 select('*') under the admin's RLS. The header L15 excludes Notes as 'owner-only personal content with NO admin bypass'. Ruling: admins reach a personal space from Files only.
- **SEC-5 [major] — partly.** No header and no version gate. A p_client_contract on provisioning is rejected too: old builds never call it. Instead the database makes stale builds harmless. Writes are owner-only (SEC-6). fn_files_personal_provider_guard refuses local_server and google_drive bodies in a personal project, so an old owner build's upload fails with a sentence instead of landing on disk. The client ships first on beta and staging. Residual to OUTSTANDING with the minimum-version question: an admin's stale desktop build lists others' personal spaces in R.A.B.B.I.T., read-only, until it updates.
  - *Evidence:* grep for min_client_version|minClientVersion|MIN_APP_VERSION|forceUpdate|required_version|client_contract across src, electron and supabase finds nothing. supabaseAdapter listProjects L2686-2695 has no filter; projectIsPrivate L336-339. RLS cannot tell builds apart. A request-header gate would also be read by the storage, realtime and Edge Function evaluations of the same policies, which do not carry the header (supabaseClient.js L311-319 sets no headers), so it would break signing and realtime.
- **SEC-6 [major] — taken.** Owner-only writes are enforced by restating four latest helper bodies with a personal arm in front, rather than with restrictive policies: one definition reaches every policy, fn_trash_authz, presign and the NAS RPC. Restated: can_write_project, can_comment_project, can_manage_project_roster (0013, personal → false) and can_edit_shot_lists (0084); grants are re-asserted as 0033 and 0084 left them. projects_delete is restated from 0013 plus personal_owner_id IS NULL. The JS mirror is in canOnProject. Probes: an admin's write, trash or upload sign on another's space raises 42501 or returns false, and the owner's succeeds. The open question of admins moving a departed person's files out is recorded (see risks).
  - *Evidence:* 0013 can_write_project L115-122 admits current_app_role() IN ('admin','manager') first; projects_update L215-222; files/folders writes (0041 L296-313, 0092) and fn_trash_authz (0092 L770+, can_write_project then passes_project_privacy, which admits admins) all let an admin write. can_presign_project_write (0051 L236-248) calls can_write_project.
- **SEC-7 [major] — taken.** The local personal space is keyed per machine and OS user. The bundle is at {userData}/personal-space/project.json and its bodies at {userData}/personal-space/files, never under the data root. A shared or copied data folder carries none. Test: two data roots on one machine give one space, and a copied data folder brings none.
  - *Evidence:* localDemoRoot.cjs checkDemoFolderShape L77-107 accepts //server/share/folder; main.cjs getRabbitDataDir L151-155 resolves under <folder>/.wilson/rabbit-data; requestSingleInstanceLock L96 is per userData; localDemoRoot header keeps files-config per machine for the copied-folder reason.
- **SEC-8 [minor] — taken.** The Moth source scan forbids, under the Moth tool folder: .from('projects'|'files'|'folders'), listPersonalSpaces, listFiles, listFolders, listManagedFiles, loadProject, listProjects, listProjectMembers and projectsIndex. One planted call per name.
  - *Evidence:* supabaseAdapter listFiles/listFolders (~L3629, L4654) and loadProject (L2735) read the base tables under the caller's RLS, so for an admin they return other people's personal-space rows.
- **SEC-9 [minor] — taken.** moth_project_files returns CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM folders fo WHERE fo.id = f.folder_id AND fo.project_id = f.project_id) THEN f.folder_id END. Probe: a file filed under a folder in someone's personal space comes back NULL. Planted: the `<>` form goes red.
  - *Evidence:* 0041 folders_select L290-294 hops under RLS, so a folder the caller cannot see reads as no row; a `<>` against NULL keeps the foreign id. moth1_core schema fact 12: no same-project folder check.
- **SEC-10 [minor] — partly.** The helper walks the folder chain from the file's folder_id up to the root, bounded at depth 64. It hides the file if any node's first path segment is a money segment, or if the top-level node (parent kind 'root') has a money slug. Checking every slug would wrongly hide working folders named 'Legal'. A writer who renames both the path and the slug of a money category can still defeat it. Pinning money category rows belongs to the reserved-folder guard (moves area interface). The residual is bounded by RLS and recorded.
  - *Evidence:* 0041 folders_update L304-313 needs only can_write_project; path and slug are free text bounded only by folders_path_shape_chk and folders_slug_shape_chk. rabbit_money_segment (0088) is upper() IN (INVOICES, FINANCE, LEGAL).
- **SEC-11 [minor] — taken.** moth_prefs is deferred to Session 2 (answer 6). Its recorded spec states that the stored map is untrusted: the reader ignores non-uuid ids, re-applies the 8 and 10 caps, re-checks every id through moth_can_read_target, and probes a directly written forged entry.
  - *Evidence:* 0098 L752-758: table_view_last's grants are table-wide, so the column is not fenced; 0046's own-row UPDATE policy lets the owner write any column.
- **PSA-01 [blocker] — taken.** The draft's edit_history_select restatement is withdrawn. Repairs D6 owns it, with a client-executable arm (admin OR can_read_project_topic(project_id), rate cards excepted). Required probes: admin and manager history reads succeed (lives_ok), and a manager reads 0 rows of a personal space.
  - *Evidence:* 0082 L148-149: REVOKE EXECUTE ON passes_project_privacy FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, with the COMMENT 'No client role executes it'. Policy functions are EXECUTE-checked as the querying role.
- **PSA-02 [major] — taken.** Hard prerequisite on repairs D6: edit_history_project_of stamps every entity type, existing rows are backfilled, and the select is fail-closed. Suite 17 probe required: a manager reads 0 rows of the personal space's 'projects' create row and of its files rows.
  - *Evidence:* 0088 COMMENT ON COLUMN edit_history.project_id: files rows only, NULL otherwise; 0092 fn_edit_history_money_snapshot runs WHEN entity_type='files'. The projects create row of ensure_personal_space holds the whole row in diff.new.
- **PSA-03 [major] — taken.** Merged with SEC-1. The fixture listProjectMemberships drops personal rows too.
  - *Evidence:* Same as SEC-1: 0020 project_members_select; TeamMembersPage load(); supabaseAdapter listProjectMembersByProjects (~L2766) reads by explicit project ids only.
- **PSA-04 [major] — taken.** Merged with SEC-4. No question is needed: her ruling 'Files view only' settles it.
  - *Evidence:* Same as SEC-4.
- **PSA-05 [major] — taken.** The personal bundle lives at {userData}/personal-space, outside rabbit-data, so archive-and-clear cannot touch it. runMigration also reads GET /api/rabbit/personal-space and adds a plain report line ('Your personal space on this computer stays on this computer; it was not moved to the cloud') to report.notCarried, so the report is not 'clean'. Test in runMigrationBins.test.js: the personal dir survives archiveLocalRabbitData.
  - *Evidence:* runMigration.js fetchLocalProjectsList L266-270 reads only GET /api/rabbit/projects. main.cjs archiveLocalRabbitData L5244-5284 rmSyncs rabbit-data/projects. resolveProjectFilesDir L2253 falls back to projects/<pid>/files.
- **PSA-06 [major] — taken.** The personal dir is outside projects/, so the DELETE containment cannot reach it. The DELETE and PATCH routes also answer 409 ('a personal space cannot be deleted or changed; its files can') when the id is the personal id. POST and PATCH strip personal, personal_owner_id and folder fields from the body. Route test: POST personal-space, DELETE it, refused, directory still present.
  - *Evidence:* main.cjs L2750-2758 DELETE /api/rabbit/projects/:id contains the id under projectsDir and rmSyncs it; PATCH L2682+ spreads the body.
- **PSA-07 [major] — taken.** One model per company. The cloud personal row is the only personal space in a workspace. In a network company its files are catalogue rows (network_share, NAS area) in <drive root>/Personal_<owner hex> (folder_slug, set at provisioning). They are added only from the desktop: a copy into that folder, then a catalogue refresh. The add dialog states the office-server exposure. The browser shows 'Add files to your personal space from the desktop app'. The signed-out per-computer space is not a workspace's and is labelled 'Personal space on this computer'.
  - *Evidence:* supabaseAdapter uploadFile ~L3484-3500 throws for a network workspace; moth1_core storage fact 8: network bodies are desktop-only today. Answer 3: 'Yes, say so' (kept on the office server, people told in plain words).
- **PSA-08 [minor] — taken.** A personal space is never the provider's active project. Moth and Files call adapter methods with an explicit projectId. Both projectsIndex merge sites refuse a record carrying personal_owner_id or is_private === true, drop the id if present and schedule the refetch. Provider test with a planted control.
  - *Evidence:* RabbitProvider.jsx L1139-1147: the project-patch effect merges any projects record into projectsIndex; handleWorkspaceEvent L1247-1277 merges with no privacy check.
- **PSA-09 [minor] — taken.** Merged with SEC-2. The fail-closed shape is pinned by repairs D7's body post-condition, because CI has no realtime.
  - *Evidence:* Same as SEC-2 (0101 L185-192 against the draft's arm).
- **PSA-10 [minor] — taken.** Personal entries are decided from the listPersonalSpaces row. canWrite = backendWrites && canOnProject({ appRole, projectRole: null, isStaffed: true, isPersonal: true, isPersonalOwner: row.personal_owner_id === me, ready, noRoles }, 'project.entity.write'). canSeeMoney and canSeeLegal = owner OR admin. Another person's space is read-only with the reason. Controls that act only on the open project stay hidden for personal entries. Mounted fixture tests.
  - *Evidence:* ProjectFilesExplorer.jsx L481-492: canWrite for a project that is not open = app admin or manager; canSeeMoney = admin. A personal space is never the open project.
- **PSA-11 [minor] — taken.** missing0084Function is generalised to missingFunction(fnName), keeping its body, and every new RPC uses it. Adapter tests for PGRST202 and a named 42883 on each.
  - *Evidence:* supabaseAdapter L1751-1758 missing0084Function: PGRST202 or a 42883 that names the function; useWorkspaceMembers MISSING_RPC_CODES.
- **PSA-12 [minor] — taken.** Stated in the dependency on repairs migration A: the comments hop is EXISTS (projects p WHERE p.id = fn_comment_project_id(entity_type, entity_id)). Personal-space comments are also owner-only through the restated can_comment_project.
  - *Evidence:* 0000 comments has entity_type/entity_id and no project_id; 0013 comments_insert uses can_comment_project(fn_comment_project_id(entity_type, entity_id)).
- **PSA-13 [minor] — taken.** Rule stated: every suite file goes into rls.yml's replay list in number order, table suites also get an RLS_TABLES line, and each commit that adds a suite edits both tail pins to that commit's real last entries, re-measured at the tip.
  - *Evidence:* rlsSuiteLists.test.js: the replay list must equal every suite file (L53-57); listed.slice(-5) (L75) and picked.slice(-6) (L111-115) are pinned to end at 109.
- **PSA-14 [minor] — taken.** moth_islands returns is_mine (personal_owner_id = auth.uid()), pinned in the return-type post-condition, and isPersonalOwner is set from it. pgTAP: an admin's moth_islands never has a row with is_personal AND NOT is_mine. Planted: drop the helper's personal arm and the probe goes red.
  - *Evidence:* Draft CL-1 ctx used isPersonalOwner: island.is_personal; MR-5 returned no owner field.

#### Decisions

- **PS-1** The personal space is a projects row: personal_owner_id uuid (no FK, the 0007/0020 person-id precedent), CHECK projects_personal_shape_chk (personal_owner_id IS NULL OR (is_private AND created_by = personal_owner_id)), and partial UNIQUE projects_personal_owner_uniq on (workspace_id, personal_owner_id) WHERE personal_owner_id IS NOT NULL. There is no new table. The classifier everywhere is personal_owner_id IS NOT NULL (SQL), plus isPersonalSpace(row) in JS (row.personal_owner_id != null, or a local bundle's project.personal === true).
  - *Why:* files.project_id is NOT NULL FK projects, and storage policies, signer, quota, GC and teardown key on projects/<id>/. is_private = true brings projects_select (0072), passes_project_privacy (0082), the 0014 hops, the topic gate (0016) and the repaired broadcasts for free. With no FK, the space survives deactivation and account deletion for the admins' Files reach.
- **PS-2** public.ensure_personal_space() RETURNS uuid. LANGUAGE plpgsql VOLATILE SECURITY DEFINER, search_path pinned, owned by a BYPASSRLS role (asserted in a 0069-style preflight). REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC, anon; GRANT TO authenticated. It refuses with 42501 when auth.uid() or current_workspace_id() is NULL or the membership is not active. It returns the existing id, or INSERTs (workspace_id, title 'Personal space', is_private true, personal_owner_id = created_by = auth.uid(), folder_slug = 'Personal_' || replace(auth.uid()::text, '-', '')) ON CONFLICT on the partial index DO NOTHING, then re-SELECTs. It creates no folder rows: folder_id NULL is the root.
  - *Why:* projects_insert is admin|manager only (0013; suite 18 probes 20-23), so a narrow RPC is needed. Under READ COMMITTED the losing racer re-reads the winner. fileSlugify only produces [A-Za-z0-9-], so 'Personal_<hex>' can never collide with a project's slug. It is set on every row so a later switch to office-server storage already has the folder. The 0049 folder_root guard needs admin|manager, which is why folder_slug is used and not folder_root. The repairs owner guard skips DEFINER bodies (current_user <> 'authenticated'), so created_by is explicit.
- **PS-3** The personal-only guard trg_projects_personal_guard (BEFORE UPDATE ON projects, fn_projects_personal_guard, plpgsql, not DEFINER, reads nothing). service_role passes. Otherwise, with 42501: personal_owner_id cannot change; on a personal row, deleted_at, folder_slug and folder_root cannot change. projects_insert is restated from 0013 plus AND personal_owner_id IS NULL. projects_delete is restated from 0013 plus AND personal_owner_id IS NULL. The created_by pin and the is_private-flip rule are NOT here: they belong to repairs D4's trg_projects_owner_guard.
  - *Why:* There is one owner guard (repairs) and one personal guard (here), so there are no two restatements of the same rule. Binning would strand the slot (the unique index covers binned rows). Hard delete would destroy the person's files, which is an admin write the read-only reach does not allow. Workspace teardown still CASCADEs. Pinning the folder keeps a walk from cataloguing another project's folder into the space. soft_delete_row is DEFINER, but the guard does not test current_user, so the binning refusal holds on the RPC path.
- **PS-4** Seats: fn_project_members_personal_guard (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON project_members, SECURITY DEFINER, search_path pinned, REVOKEd from PUBLIC, anon and authenticated) refuses with 42501 any seat on a personal project other than (personal_owner_id, 'manager'). DELETE is not guarded.
  - *Why:* fn_projects_auto_staff (0020) is DEFINER and fires AFTER UPDATE OF producer_id: an owner setting producer_id would otherwise seat a colleague, and its EXCEPTION swallows the refusal as a WARNING. Client seat writes are already refused by the restated can_manage_project_roster. The account-deletion CASCADE must not be blocked.
- **PS-5** The personal arm in the four project helpers, restating the latest bodies (can_write_project, can_comment_project and can_manage_project_roster from 0013; can_edit_shot_lists from 0084): SELECT CASE WHEN (SELECT p.personal_owner_id FROM projects p WHERE p.id = p_project) IS NOT NULL THEN <personal> ELSE <latest body verbatim> END. <personal> is COALESCE((SELECT p.personal_owner_id = auth.uid() FROM projects p WHERE p.id = p_project), false) for write, comment and shot lists, and false for roster. Grants are re-asserted (0033 L195-202; 0084 L191-192). Each COMMENT names the projectRoleMatrix.js lockstep.
  - *Why:* SEC-6: admins reach the space read-only from Files. One definition covers every write policy, fn_trash_authz, can_presign_project_write, the files and storage write policies, and the NAS catalogue apply. Seats no longer matter on a personal row, so an orphaned (unstaffed) space is not opened to members, and personal spaces do not depend on the A-R2-03 hops. The ELSE branch keeps the 0013 NULL semantics exactly.
- **PS-6** Media routing. supabaseAdapter.projectIsPrivate answers is_private AND personal_owner_id IS NULL (personal_owner_id is selected only when the personalSpacesAvailable probe says the column exists), so a personal upload takes the workspace-choice branch: petal → supabase, s3 → s3, money pinned to supabase. A new fn_files_personal_provider_guard (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF project_id, storage_provider ON files; SECURITY DEFINER; search_path pinned; REVOKEd) refuses with 42501 a row whose project is personal and whose storage_provider::text IN ('local_server','google_drive'). It compares as text, so it does not depend on the new 'network_share' enum value.
  - *Why:* Ruling: company's chosen storage. uploadFile measured routing every private non-money upload to LOCAL_SERVER. The guard makes a stale build's upload fail instead of landing on someone's disk (SEC-5). It enforces answer 1 at the database: a private project's local body reaches a personal space only once it has been copied up. The bodies area may absorb this rule into fn_files_body_guard, provided it stays one definition.
- **PS-7** Office-server companies (answer 3). The personal space's folder is <drive root>/Personal_<owner hex> (folder_slug; network_share_folder_of resolves it). In the cloud, uploadFile's network refusal stays. On the desktop, Moth's 'Add files' for the owner's space calls the new route POST /api/network-share/personal-space/files (launch token). It takes { root, folderSlug, name } plus the body stream. It requires folderSlug to match ^Personal_[0-9a-f]{32}$ and to equal the signed-in user's hex, and it checks strict containment under root with pathContainment. It writes tmp then rename, never overwrites (it names ' (2)'), and confirms the size. Then the renderer runs the NAS area's walk for that project (network_share_catalogue_apply, authority through the restated can_write_project, so owner only). The add dialog always shows: 'This company keeps its files on its office server. Files you add to your personal space are kept there, in a folder named Personal_…. WILSON shows them only to you (and to workspace admins in Files), but anyone who opens the server directly can find them.' The browser shows 'Add files to your personal space from the desktop app.'
  - *Why:* Her answer 3 is verbatim 'Yes, say so', so the file has to be addable and the person told. No body is uploaded, which keeps the NAS area's metadata-only rule. The folder name is unforgeable by titles and pinned by PS-3, and other projects' walks skip it (NAS NS-D8).
- **PS-8** Kept out of ordinary lists. supabaseAdapter.listProjects adds .is('personal_owner_id', null) behind the probe. Local GET /api/rabbit/projects never sees the personal bundle (it lives outside projects/). Both projectsIndex merge sites (handleWorkspaceEvent and project-patch) refuse is_private or personal rows and refetch. A personal space is never opened as the active project. TeamMembersPage drops null and personal embeds. WorkspaceTakeout excludes personal rows and their children. googleDriveAdapter: ensurePersonalSpace throws its readOnly() sentence, and listPersonalSpaces returns [].
  - *Why:* Measured: 12 projectsIndex consumers, plus two direct readers outside it (Team, Takeout). Filtering at the sources covers all of them.
- **PS-9** Files view. ProjectFilesExplorer (showPicker only) loads adapter.listPersonalSpaces() → [{ id, title, personal_owner_id, folder_slug, updated_at }]. The entries are 'My personal space', or for admins 'Personal space · <display name>' (useWorkspaceMembers) with '(inactive)' or '(former member)'. Gates per PSA-10. When an admin selects another person's space, the explorer calls adapter.recordPersonalSpaceOpened(id) once per selection before listing it. A failure shows a sentence and does not list the space.
  - *Why:* Ruling: owner plus admins in Files only. projects_select already admits admins and the children follow the hop, so no new read is needed. Answer 4 (see AU-1).
- **AU-1** Answer 4. public.record_personal_space_opened(p_project uuid) RETURNS void: SECURITY DEFINER, search_path pinned, owned by a BYPASSRLS role (preflight as 0069), REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon, GRANT authenticated. It raises one 42501 message unless the caller is an active member with current_app_role() = 'admin' and p_project is a personal row in current_workspace_id() with personal_owner_id <> auth.uid(). It inserts app_events (workspace_id, actor_user_id = auth.uid(), event_type 'admin', code 'WIL-4114' (re-measure: 4101-4108 and 4110-4113 are taken, 4109 is unused, so skip it), severity 'info', message 'An admin opened someone else''s personal space in Files', context { project_id, owner_user_id }), ids only, under the TPN note. errorCodes.js registers WIL-4114, and LogsSection (admin-only by app_events_select) shows it.
  - *Why:* app_events_insert (0021 L99-108) refuses event_type 'admin' and WIL-41xx to clients, so only a DEFINER can mint the line, which makes it evidence (the 0069 WIL-4108 precedent). RLS cannot log SELECTs, so a raw REST read by an admin is not recorded. That is stated in the handbook deviations and in OUTSTANDING.
- **MR-1** Moth reads only through SECURITY INVOKER RPCs, each filtered by moth_can_read_target: moth_islands(), moth_project_folders(p_project), moth_project_files(p_project, p_after uuid, p_limit int clamped 1..1000, keyset on id). A hidden or missing project gives an empty result, never an error, and there are no counts or totals anywhere. The widened Moth source scan applies (SEC-8).
  - *Why:* RLS cannot tell Moth from Files, and admins keep RLS reach for Files. An INVOKER RPC can only narrow.
- **MR-2** public._moth_target_eligible(p_kind text, p_id uuid, p_allow_trashed boolean): SECURITY DEFINER, EXECUTE revoked from PUBLIC, anon and authenticated, so only definers call it (moves area's moth_trash_index with true). moth_can_read_target(p_kind, p_id) = _moth_target_eligible(p_kind, p_id, false), GRANT authenticated. Session 1 kinds are project, folder and file. Any other kind, a NULL id, or no auth, workspace or active membership gives false. Kind only picks the table, and the decision comes from the row found.
  - *Why:* Later Moth tables' policies call it, and DEFINER avoids recursion. Missing and hidden both answer false, so it is no oracle beyond the recorded same-project receipt residual.
- **MR-3** Checks, from the row only. Folder: same workspace, and its chain (PSA/SEC-10 walk: no node with a money first path segment, and no top-level node with a money slug), then its project. File: same workspace; deleted_at IS NULL unless p_allow_trashed; NOT file_row_is_money(is_financial, storage_path); NOT 'legal' = ANY(tags); its folder chain passes as above; NOT EXISTS (expenses e WHERE e.project_id = f.project_id AND e.workspace_id = f.workspace_id AND e.file_ids @> ARRAY[f.id]). Project: same workspace, deleted_at IS NULL, passes_project_privacy(p.id), AND (p.personal_owner_id IS NULL OR p.personal_owner_id = auth.uid()). The personal arm has no admin leg.
  - *Why:* Money is excluded for every role through the classifiers, never the access gates. The receipt arm is scoped to the file's own project (SEC-3). Moves refuse expense members, so receipts never leave their project.
- **MR-4** moth_can_write_target(p_kind, p_id) = moth_can_read_target(p_kind, p_id) AND can_write_project(<resolved project>). SECURITY DEFINER, GRANT authenticated.
  - *Why:* With the PS-5 personal arm, can_write_project already means owner-only on personal rows and the usual rule elsewhere, so there is no second write rule.
- **MR-5** moth_islands() RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, title text, is_personal boolean, is_mine boolean, is_private boolean, my_project_role text, is_staffed boolean, can_write boolean, updated_at timestamptz). moth_project_folders returns TABLE(id, parent_id, kind, entity_type, slug, label, path, sort_order, updated_at). moth_project_files returns TABLE(id, project_id, folder_id, name, mime_type, size_bytes, kind, document_kind, description, tags, storage_provider, storage_path, thumbnail_url, duration_sec, source_modified_at, uploaded_at, updated_at), with folder_id in the SEC-9 positive form. Post-conditions pin all three result types: no is_financial, no created_by, no count.
  - *Why:* The seat inputs come from the server (no caller-supplied pairs). is_mine closes PSA-14. Provider and path are already readable through files_select and are what the preview needs.
- **CL-1** No new action. canOnProject gains ctx.isPersonal and ctx.isPersonalOwner, decided right after the ready === false check: when isPersonal, project.entity.write, project.comment.write, project.shotlist.write, project.bins.write and project.settings.open return isPersonalOwner === true, and project.roster.manage returns false. project.shotlist.activate and project.bins.vet keep their rules (their SQL is not restated). projectActionDeniedReason adds 'Only the owner of a personal space can change it.' Moth builds ctx inline from an island row: { appRole, projectRole: island.my_project_role, isStaffed: island.is_staffed, isPersonal: island.is_personal, isPersonalOwner: island.is_mine, ready, noRoles }. Denied controls are shown through GatedAction.
  - *Why:* It mirrors PS-5 action for action, stays under writeGate.test.js's ready scan, and adds no new export.
- **CL-2** Lockstep is pinned by one table. The read-path suite carries '-- MATRIX <action> | appRole | projectRole | staffed | personal | owner | expected' lines for entity.write, comment.write, roster.manage and shotlist.write, executed against can_write_project, can_comment_project, can_manage_project_roster and can_edit_shot_lists (and moth_can_write_target for entity.write) with fixture users. projectRoleMatrix.test.js parses the same file and asserts canOnProject agrees on every row. mothFileHidden(row, { expenseFileIds, localMoneyDirs }) in the Moth tool folder mirrors the file arms: is_financial, isMoneyKey, the folder-chain arms, the 'legal' tag, expense ids and the desktop {slug}_RECEIPTS&INVOICES, {slug}_CREWINVOICES and {slug}_TALENTINVOICES directories. A vitest reads the helper migration text, comments stripped, and asserts the same arms, including e.project_id = f.project_id.
  - *Why:* No test compares SQL with JS today (moth1_core schema fact 13). On the signed-out desktop there is no RLS.
- **DT-1** Signed-out desktop: one personal space per computer and OS user, labelled 'Personal space on this computer'. The bundle is at {userData}/personal-space/project.json ({ project: { id: <uuid minted once>, title: 'Personal space', personal: true } }), and its bodies at {userData}/personal-space/files. Routes: GET /api/rabbit/personal-space, and POST /api/rabbit/personal-space (check-and-create in one synchronous handler). getRabbitProjectDir(id) resolves the personal id to that directory, so the existing files, folders and managed-files routes work. resolveProjectFolder(personal) returns null (no folder and no _DATABASES mirror under the data root), and resolveProjectFilesDir(personal) returns {userData}/personal-space/files. DELETE and PATCH /api/rabbit/projects/:id answer 409 for the personal id, and POST and PATCH strip personal, personal_owner_id, folder_slug, folder_root and files_dir. runMigration reports it as not carried. archiveLocalRabbitData cannot reach it.
  - *Why:* SEC-7, PSA-05 and PSA-06. userData is per OS user, and a signed-out desktop has no workspace identity beyond the machine user. Answer 2's real-file moves use resolveProjectFilesDir, which includes this directory.
- **TK-1** Takeout excludes personal spaces and every child row (SEC-4 rule), and its manifest says so.
  - *Why:* Ruling: admins reach a personal space from Files only, the same reason Notes are excluded.
- **A6-1** Answer 6: user_settings.moth_prefs (last view, appearance, recent folders, first visit) is deferred to Session 2 with the draft US-1 spec plus SEC-11. moth_prefs jsonb object ≤ 32768, keyed by workspace with member_since = workspace_members.created_at. Writer set_moth_prefs(p_patch) is INVOKER; the reader moth_prefs() is INVOKER STABLE and treats the map as untrusted: non-uuid ids dropped, caps re-applied, every id re-checked through moth_can_read_target, last_view kept only if it is a quick token or a moth_views row the caller can SELECT. Suite 57 is extended then. Session 1 creates no Moth table in this area and no moth_local_scopes.
  - *Why:* Her answer: views and layouts in Session 2; last_view points at moth_views. Session 1's acceptance (items 1-6) needs no preferences.

#### Schema changes

- **public.projects.personal_owner_id, projects_personal_shape_chk, projects_personal_owner_uniq** (restates from: new): ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS personal_owner_id uuid. ADD CONSTRAINT projects_personal_shape_chk CHECK (personal_owner_id IS NULL OR (is_private AND created_by = personal_owner_id)), in a guarded DO block. CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS projects_personal_owner_uniq ON projects (workspace_id, personal_owner_id) WHERE personal_owner_id IS NOT NULL. A COMMENT names ensure_personal_space and 'owner-only in Moth; owner plus workspace admins (read-only) in Files'. Post-conditions: the column exists, pg_get_constraintdef has both arms, the index is unique and partial, zero rows violate.
- **public.fn_projects_personal_guard() + trg_projects_personal_guard BEFORE UPDATE ON projects** (restates from: new (the created_by pin and is_private rule stay in repairs D4 trg_projects_owner_guard)): plpgsql, not DEFINER, REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated. IF current_setting('role', true) = 'service_role' THEN RETURN NEW. Raise 42501 when NEW.personal_owner_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.personal_owner_id. When OLD.personal_owner_id IS NOT NULL, raise 42501 on any change to deleted_at ('a personal space cannot be put in the bin; its files can'), folder_slug or folder_root. The post-condition (comments stripped) names all four columns.
- **policies projects_insert, projects_delete** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql (re-measure: repairs migration A restates projects_update only; if it also restates either of these, merge into that body)): DROP and CREATE each, restating 0013 word for word plus AND personal_owner_id IS NULL. Post-conditions compare the quals with comments stripped: every 0013 arm plus the new one.
- **public.can_write_project, can_comment_project, can_manage_project_roster (0013); can_edit_shot_lists (0084)** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql; 0084_shot_lists_and_edits.sql): CREATE OR REPLACE with the PS-5 CASE: the personal arm first, the latest body verbatim in ELSE. Personal arm: owner for write, comment and shot lists; false for roster. LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public. Re-assert REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon and GRANT TO authenticated, service_role (0033 L195-202; 0084 L191-192). COMMENTs restate the lockstep line. Post-conditions on the comment-stripped bodies: each contains personal_owner_id = auth.uid() (roster: the false arm) and its original arms; authenticated still has EXECUTE; anon does not.
- **public.fn_project_members_personal_guard() + trigger BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON project_members** (restates from: new): SECURITY DEFINER, search_path pinned, REVOKEd from PUBLIC, anon, authenticated. v_owner := (SELECT personal_owner_id FROM projects WHERE id = NEW.project_id). IF v_owner IS NOT NULL AND (NEW.user_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_owner OR NEW.project_role <> 'manager') THEN RAISE 42501 ('a personal space has one seat, its owner''s').
- **public.fn_files_personal_provider_guard() + trigger BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF project_id, storage_provider ON files** (restates from: new): SECURITY DEFINER, search_path pinned, REVOKEd. IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM projects p WHERE p.id = NEW.project_id AND p.personal_owner_id IS NOT NULL) AND NEW.storage_provider::text IN ('local_server','google_drive') THEN RAISE 42501 ('a personal space keeps its files in the company''s storage'). The bodies area may fold this into fn_files_body_guard as one definition.
- **public.ensure_personal_space() RETURNS uuid** (restates from: new): Per PS-2. A preflight asserts the owner has BYPASSRLS. Post-conditions: prosecdef, proconfig search_path, no anon or PUBLIC EXECUTE, authenticated EXECUTE.
- **public.record_personal_space_opened(p_project uuid) RETURNS void** (restates from: new (0069 otter_nomination_apply audit-insert shape)): Per AU-1. A BYPASSRLS-owner preflight (0069 shape). Post-condition: the app_events_insert qual still reserves event_type 'admin' and WIL-41.
- **expenses_file_ids_gin** (restates from: new): CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS expenses_file_ids_gin ON public.expenses USING gin (file_ids). 0037 has only expenses_project_idx.
- **public._moth_target_eligible / moth_can_read_target / moth_can_write_target** (restates from: new): plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public, per MR-2 to MR-4. The folder-chain check is a WITH RECURSIVE up (id, parent_id, kind, slug, path, depth) from the folder id following parent_id, with depth < 64. It fails when any node with kind <> 'root' has rabbit_money_segment(split_part(path,'/',1)), or the node whose parent is the root has rabbit_money_segment(slug). Grants: _moth_target_eligible revoked from PUBLIC, anon and authenticated; the other two revoked from PUBLIC and anon and granted to authenticated. Post-conditions on the comment-stripped bodies: file_row_is_money, rabbit_money_segment(, passes_project_privacy, 'e.project_id = f.project_id', 'personal_owner_id = auth.uid()' with no current_app_role in the personal arm, can_write_project in the write twin.
- **public.moth_islands() / moth_project_folders(uuid) / moth_project_files(uuid, uuid, int)** (restates from: new): LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY INVOKER SET search_path = public. Per MR-1 and MR-5. moth_islands filters with moth_can_read_target('project', id) and computes is_mine = personal_owner_id = auth.uid(). REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon; GRANT authenticated. Post-conditions pin pg_get_function_result for all three.

#### Migration order

Repairs B: can_access_project_money privacy arm; edit_history_project_of with backfill and a client-executable fail-closed edit_history_select. Repairs C: the fail-closed two-sided workspace broadcast with AUDIENCE. Repairs D11: the project_members_select and task_templates_select hops.

- **P1 personal space (next free number after P0, re-measured)**: Column, CHECK, index; projects_insert and projects_delete restated; trg_projects_personal_guard; the four helper restatements; the project_members personal guard; fn_files_personal_provider_guard; ensure_personal_space; record_personal_space_opened; preflights and post-conditions.

Suites 01_projects, 18_project_members, 80_private_projects, 25_app_events and the shot-list suite (84) are extended in the same commit, with plan counts updated and collected = planned. 35_platform_audit passes. The client ships first everywhere (it is probe-guarded).
