- **Trash RPCs** (provides): moth_trash_files(uuid[]) and moth_restore_files(uuid[]) RETURN integer. moth_trash_index(p_limit int DEFAULT 200, p_before timestamptz DEFAULT NULL) RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, project_id uuid, project_title text, folder_id uuid, name text, mime_type text, size_bytes bigint, kind file_kind, deleted_at timestamptz, deleted_by_label text, purges_at timestamptz, can_restore boolean, needs_desktop boolean).
- **Trusted-writer predicate for the files column guards** (provides): public.fn_checked_move_admits(p_file uuid, p_project uuid, p_folder uuid, p_provider text, p_path text) RETURNS boolean, plus GUC wilson.file_move = the file_moves.id written in the current txid. The bodies guard (fn_files_body_guard) and the NAS guard (fn_files_network_share_guard) must admit a change of project_id, folder_id, storage_provider, storage_path or thumbnail_url by a non-client role inside a signed-in call only when this returns true, rekey_file_body's marker applies, or the catalogue writer GUC is on. RI ON DELETE SET NULL of folder_id stays admitted.
- **Body provider values** (provides): fn_moth_body_provider_for(ws) RETURNS 'supabase'|'s3'|'network_share'. A move writes storage_provider only for copy_up items (local_server → that value) and share_move items (it stays network_share). Moves refuse local_server cross-project except as a copy-up into a shared project or the caller's personal space, and refuse google_drive.
- **file_events vocabulary** (provides): Union, set by one CHECK: uploaded, downloaded, moved, relinked, trashed, restored, purged, upload_abandoned, viewed_remote, catalogued (NAS), moved_out, moved_in (bodies), refiled (this area: details {from_folder_id, to_folder_id}, same project only). On a project change: old_path and new_path are NULL, details '{}', and no 'moved' row. The desktop bundle.fileEvents adds trashed, restored, refiled, moved_out and moved_in locally.
- **Realtime project-change split** (provides): In fn_realtime_broadcast, after the v_project NULL return: an UPDATE whose project_id changes gives DELETE(OLD) on the old topic and INSERT(NEW) on the new topic. No 'files' literal. Merged into one restatement with the NAS area's network_share_writer early return and network_share_checks arm.
- **Desktop routes** (provides): Local Server mode: DELETE …/files/:id (trash); POST …/files/:id/restore; POST …/managed-files/:id/restore; POST …/files/trash {ids}; POST …/files/restore {ids}; GET …/trash; POST …/trash/purge-expired; POST …/files/move {moves:[{id,to_folder_id}]}; POST /api/rabbit/files/move-between-projects {from_project_id,to_project_id,moves}.
Cloud desktop: POST /api/rabbit/network-share/{move,bin,unbin,sweep-bin}; POST local-media retire; GET pending move journals. All behind applyLocalServerLock.
- **Personal-space classifier and gates** (needs): From the personal area: projects.personal_owner_id with projects_personal_shape_chk (is_private=true AND created_by=personal_owner_id) and PS-3's created_by pin and immutable owner. _moth_target_eligible(p_kind text, p_id uuid, p_allow_trashed boolean) callable by definers. moth_can_write_target(p_kind, p_id). moth_islands() rows {id, title, is_personal, is_private, my_project_role, is_staffed, can_write}. The 'project.moth.write' canOnProject action. PS-6, so personal uploads go to company storage. A NAS folder (folder_root) for personal spaces in office-server companies.
- **Stable-body contract** (needs): From the bodies area: the 8 base storage policies restated to follow the current project (the pre-flight checks storage_object_current_project in their text). rabbit_file_key_admissible(provider, path, project, file, folder, financial) for copy-up keys. moved_out and moved_in arms with NULL paths and '{}' details. The storage_gc_queue thumbnail-detach contract (reason 'file-purged', kind 'thumbnail'). Their guard calling fn_checked_move_admits.
- **Office-server catalogue** (needs): From the NAS area: the 'network_share' enum value, committed. network_share_locator_ok(text). network_share_folder_of(folder_root, folder_slug, root). Their guard admitting storage_path changes through fn_checked_move_admits. The walk keeps skipping dot folders (.wilson-bin). NS-D5/D8's moved-in-row handling dropped, since moves are physical. The purge-certificate detail reworded to 'in the company server's bin until a desktop removes it'. workspaceRootDir and rootReachable in main, for the bin and move routes. A sweep-bin call on each walk.
- **Workspace-channel suppression** (needs): files stays off fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast (repairs D7). Moves change no projects row, so no workspace event is produced.
- **History capture** (needs): From the tables area: the fn_moth_history_capture WHEN clause on files includes project_id, folder_id and deleted_at, so a move gives one 'move' row, and a trash or restore gives 'trash' or 'restore', with each side's audience snapshotted. It uses the same exclusion as _moth_target_eligible. From the repairs area: edit_history_select gains AND (entity_type <> 'files' OR NOT (diff ? 'project_id') OR can_read_project_topic(fn_try_uuid(diff->'project_id'->>'old'))).

### 2.4 The office-NAS catalogue and desktop-only projects

Findings: 7 partly, 25 taken.

**Scope as resolved:** office-NAS catalogue and desktop-only projects

**Summary:** REVISED design (replaces the draft). Office-server (NAS) files are public.files rows with a new body provider value 'network_share' (added in its own migration). Their locator is storage_path = 'nas:' + a forward-slash path relative to the company drive. Her answer 5 ('it acts like a file manager') changes the draft's read-only model. The desktop now really moves, bins, restores and adds files on the server. That gives one invariant: a live NAS row's file always lies inside its own space's folder. Moving a row moves the file. Deleting moves the file into a per-space bin folder '<space folder>/.wilson-bin/<file id>/'. Restore brings it back. Once the cloud's 30-day purge has written the deletion certificate, the desktop removes it for good. A browser lists rows and says each action is done from the desktop app.

A space's folder is either projects.folder_root, which only an admin or manager can set (0049), placed strictly inside a UNC drive, or a fixed personal folder '_WILSON_PERSONAL/<owner uuid>'. There is no slug or title fallback (NS-SEC-01). Folder claims of every project in any state, including slugs, plus the reserved personal folder, are checked fail-closed: nesting is refused when folders are set, and an apply refuses rather than skips (NS-SEC-02/06/07). Only definer RPCs write NAS rows. They work under a writer GUC bound to the project ('check:<id>') or the row ('op:<id>') and cleared before return (NS-X03). A guard trigger refuses every other change to a NAS row's provider, locator, metadata, trash state, project or folder.

The check is a worker-thread walk with async containment and same-origin plus token gates. Frame sequences are collapsed. Batching is stateless: each directory carries its own record of completeness and subfolders, and the missing sweep runs per directory (NS-X05/X07). Only people who hold the project's roster seat run checks (or the owner, for a personal space). Anyone who can edit runs file operations. Each file operation is server-first and journalled in network_share_ops, then settled by the desktop, so a crash never duplicates or loses a row. 'Last checked' lives in network_share_checks, which has its own thin broadcast (NS-X01). Catalogue writes depend on the repairs area's edit_history privacy arm (NS-SEC-03). moth_local_scopes and local Moth state are deferred to Session 2, with a question for the owner (answer 6, NS-X09).

Two open questions for the owner are recorded under decisions NS-Q1 and NS-Q2. Note: while extracting the reviews, one scratch file was written outside the repo, in the session's scratch folder. No repo file was touched.

#### Findings and their dispositions

- **NS-SEC-01 [blocker] — taken.** The catalogue folder comes from folder_root (0049 seat) or the fixed personal folder only, and the slug arm is removed (NS-D2). Slugs remain as claims only (NS-D8). The new projects guard refuses a change to a set folder_slug unless COALESCE(current_app_role() IN (admin,manager),false) or service_role holds, and a shape CHECK keeps the slug to one segment. Probes: a member's slug UPDATE throws; 'a/b' violates the CHECK.
  - *Evidence:* 0041 adds folder_slug TEXT with no CHECK. 0013 projects_update USING can_write_project, and its unstaffed arm admits every member. 0049 fn_project_folder_root_guard examines NEW.folder_root only. The CREATE TRIGGER trg_projects_* list has no slug guard.
- **NS-SEC-02 [blocker] — taken.** network_share_claims(ws) returns every project in any state. An unplaceable folder_root is returned as an unplaced claim, and the apply then refuses 'folders_unplaced' after the authority step. The Admin Storage page lists those projects to admins, who already see every project (0072). A folder equal to, containing or inside another claim refuses 'folder_shared'. Planted control: treating an unplaced claim as no claim turns its probe red.
  - *Evidence:* main.cjs resolveProjectFolder l.1848-1860 (folder_root, else root plus folder_slug or fileSlugify(title)). 0049 lets service_role through and never re-validates a stored value after the root changes. 0048 lets an admin repoint root_path.
- **NS-SEC-03 [blocker] — taken.** NS-2's pre-flight RAISEs unless the repairs area's Migration B privacy arm is live in edit_history_select (anchor: passes_project_privacy, or that area's named projects hop). The catalogue suite probes that a non-creator manager reads 0 history rows for a private project's or a personal space's NAS rows.
  - *Evidence:* 0092 edit_history_select (l.1017-1028) checks workspace, app_role admin/manager, membership and the money arm, with no project privacy. 0012 captures full rows on files.
- **NS-SEC-04 [major] — taken.** Every NAS RPC requires mode='byos', provider='network' and root_kind='unc' ('drive_changed' otherwise). networkShare.cjs refuses unless workspaceRootDir starts with '//'. Client eligibility checks the same. Probes cover a local root on the server side and a drive-letter root on the route.
  - *Evidence:* 0048 workspace_storage_kind_chk admits 'local'. main.cjs rabbit:set-workspace-root (l.5436-5445) applies a local root wherever the path exists.
- **NS-SEC-05 [major] — partly.** Checks are limited to can_manage_project_roster plus privacy (canOnProject 'project.roster.manage'), the people who set up folders, or to the owner for a personal space. A folder the checker cannot read (EACCES/EPERM) is sent as incomplete, so nothing under it is marked missing. The Storage page and the Files banner say the list shows what the person who checked could open. The whole question goes to the owner as NS-Q1.
  - *Evidence:* 0013 can_write_project admits every member seat and, on unstaffed projects, every member. files_select (0092) has no seat arm. Server-side permissions per person cannot be read from metadata.
- **NS-SEC-06 [major] — taken.** A new SECURITY DEFINER guard on projects refuses a folder_root, folder_slug or personal_owner_id that is equal to, inside or containing another claim or the reserved personal folder. The apply fails closed on legacy nesting ('folder_shared') instead of skipping. Probe: creating private P inside A's folder is refused. Planted control: dropping the guard turns it red.
  - *Evidence:* 0049 checks containment in the root only. In the draft, steps 6, 9 and 10 skip, DO NOTHING and leave stale.
- **NS-SEC-07 [major] — taken.** One function, network_share_claims, includes personal spaces (personal_owner_id) and the reserved parent '_WILSON_PERSONAL'. Both the nesting guard and the apply read only that function. A post-condition and a probe cover a project folder set to contain '_WILSON_PERSONAL'.
  - *Evidence:* In the draft, step 6 built the skip from projects only. The personal-space interface had no mechanism.
- **NS-SEC-08 [minor] — partly.** Two changes remove the cross-space hold: moving a row now moves the file, and trashed files leave the folder, so the unique index covers only live rows. The only remaining silent skip is a locator that an unsettled operation names, which is transient. It is written into the handbook as accepted. The apply's counts cover this project's rows only.
  - *Evidence:* The draft's unique index included trashed rows, and moved rows kept locators under another project's folder.
- **NS-SEC-09 [minor] — taken.** The GUC is 'check:<project>' or 'op:<file>', set per block and reset before RETURN. The guard compares it to OLD/NEW.project_id or id. A post-condition checks for the reset. Probe: a direct INSERT after an apply, in the same transaction, is refused 42501.
  - *Evidence:* 0069 l.205-237 and 0084 l.956/958 bind the GUC to an id and reset it to ''.
- **NS-SEC-10 [minor] — taken.** Every network-share route requires isSameOriginRequest and the token, and fails closed with 403. Containment uses an async realpath (NS-D7). Tests cover cross-origin and same-site requests.
  - *Evidence:* fileReads.cjs isSameOriginRequest (l.29-36). localMedia.cjs insideByRealPath (l.116-128) is synchronous.
- **NS-SEC-11 [minor] — taken.** Checks need the roster seat. A project may hold at most 50,000 NAS rows in any state ('too_many'). There are at most 60 apply calls per project in any 10 minutes, counted in network_share_checks under the advisory lock ('too_soon'). record_added takes at most 50 entries per call. All of these are probed.
  - *Evidence:* 0013's unstaffed arm. In the draft, rows were never deleted by checks.
- **NS-SEC-12 [minor] — partly.** NS-D17 is not built in Session 1. Its deferred spec carries this rule: never a field of bundle.project, only a top-level key or a userData file, with a mirror test.
  - *Evidence:* main.cjs mirrorProjectDatabases (l.2173-2182) writes bundle.project to <slug>_DATABASES/project.json.
- **NS-X01 [blocker] — taken.** network_share_checks gets its own fn_network_share_checks_broadcast, sending {id: project_id, project_id}, so the trigger set stays at 18. The files-arm early return is restated once together with the moves area's arm change. The same commit restates 101/18, 100/7 and 95/170, each with a dated comment, and the tests name them.
  - *Evidence:* 101 probes 17/18, 100 probe 7, 95 probe 170, 72 l.202-211 and 99 l.329-335 pin the md5, the arm and the 18-table trigger set. 0101 fn_budget_versions_broadcast is the thin precedent.
- **NS-X02 [blocker] — taken.** There is no temp table: CTEs over jsonb_array_elements are used. A probe calls the apply twice in one transaction.
  - *Evidence:* In the draft, step 6 used CREATE TEMP TABLE _ns ON COMMIT DROP. Suites run as one transaction.
- **NS-X03 [major] — taken.** Merged with NS-SEC-09.
  - *Evidence:* Same as NS-SEC-09 (0069 l.205-237).
- **NS-X04 [major] — taken.** The unique index now covers live rows only. The rename match excludes entries named by any live row or any pending operation in the workspace. Updates likewise match only this project's live rows. Probes cover a trashed twin plus a rename, and a pending move plus a rename.
  - *Evidence:* In the draft, step 8 checked uniqueness only within this project. The index includes trashed rows. 0014's purge waits 30 days.
- **NS-X05 [major] — taken.** Batching is stateless and per directory. Each call carries whole directories {d, complete, subdirs, files}. Missing marks apply only to rows directly in a complete directory, or under a subfolder that has vanished from one, so a split run is correct. A rename across calls may lose an id, which is a stated residual.
  - *Evidence:* In the draft, steps 8 and 10 read only the current call.
- **NS-X06 [major] — taken.** The new async shareContain() does one fs.promises.realpath of the root per reachability window and runs the candidate's realpath and stat through STAT_MAX slots with the ROOT_WAIT_MS race. It never walks ancestors. The 'share' arm of rabbit:open-path is resolved by shareContain before openOrReveal. A sync-fs spy test runs during GET and the open IPC.
  - *Evidence:* localMedia.cjs insideByRealPath l.116-131. openPath.cjs resolveOpenTarget l.87-92 uses sync calls. rabbitBins.cjs statKind is the async, bounded pattern.
- **NS-X07 [major] — partly.** A sequence folder becomes one entry: locator = the folder, mime 'inode/x-frame-sequence', size = the sum, mtime = the max. It is classified by a pure classifier shared with bins and pinned by a parity test. Per-call limits are 5,000 entries and 2,000 directories. The walk rotates from network_share_checks.next_start, so a capped walk reaches every directory over successive checks. No cursor is stored.
  - *Evidence:* rabbitBins.cjs detectSequence (l.202-260) treats one folder of frames as one item.
- **NS-X08 [major] — partly.** Merged with NS-SEC-05. The question is NS-Q1.
  - *Evidence:* Same as NS-SEC-05.
- **NS-X09 [major] — taken.** NS-D17 is withdrawn from Session 1. The choice goes to the owner as NS-Q2 before Session 2. No 'moth' bundle key is written.
  - *Evidence:* moth1_rules item 43 (§12.8). PLAN_MOTH l.115 'new Moth databases stay Supabase'. Answer 6 lists no local store for Session 1.
- **NS-X10 [major] — taken.** Merged with NS-SEC-03.
  - *Evidence:* Same as NS-SEC-03.
- **NS-X11 [major] — taken.** The post-condition asserts only that the trigger exists. Behaviour is proved by purging a NAS row and finding storage_gc_queue unchanged.
  - *Evidence:* 0054 l.150-157 'IN (supabase, s3)'. 0054's own post-condition matches only '%thumbnail_url%' (l.241-247).
- **NS-X12 [minor] — taken.** The planted controls are now: GRANT INSERT plus a permissive policy together; drop files_network_share_shape_chk and assert the conname of the CHECK that refuses; make step 7 also undelete, and assert the trashed row stays unchanged.
  - *Evidence:* network_share_checks grants SELECT only. 0050 files_money_provider_chk.
- **NS-X13 [minor] — taken.** last_checked_at is clock_timestamp(). Probes check files_seen and last_checked_by after a second caller's apply.
  - *Evidence:* 0001 fn_audit_touch l.209-212 uses now().
- **NS-X14 [minor] — taken.** All three new suites join the replay list in number order, and both tail pins are restated.
  - *Evidence:* rlsSuiteLists.test.js l.53-58, l.75 (slice -5) and l.111-115 (slice -6).
- **NS-X15 [minor] — taken.** folder_id is joined by (project_id, lower(path)) with no deleted filter.
  - *Evidence:* 0041 public.folders has no deleted_at.
- **NS-X16 [minor] — taken.** The walk takes a relative locator that has passed checkShareLocator (never empty), so it can never name the root itself. An equality test is added.
  - *Evidence:* pathContainment.cjs isPathInside l.51-56 accepts equality. main.cjs folderRootRefusal l.365-372 refuses it.
- **NS-X17 [minor] — taken.** Only the question machinery is extracted (askRootInWorker, the slots, rootAnswers, rootReachable, markOff(root)). Bins keeps its location mapping. mountCloudBins builds its own instance when any cloudBins* seam is present.
  - *Evidence:* rabbitBins.cjs l.1704-1800: the seats, cloudBins* seams, and rootKnownOffFor/markRootOffFor over cloudLocations.
- **NS-X18 [minor] — partly.** There is no slug fallback. A project without folder_root says 'An admin or manager sets this project's folder on the company server first'. The claim that a repoint keeps locators is withdrawn. StorageSection warns with a count of listed files before a root change.
  - *Evidence:* main.cjs resolveProjectFolder uses folder_slug or fileSlugify(title). supabaseAdapter l.485: cloud mode never writes folder_slug.
- **NS-X19 [minor] — partly.** D.O.G. references mark NAS rows 'On the company server'. On the web they show the desktop sentence. On the desktop a read over 50 MB (size_bytes) is refused before get. A test pins both. The two money views are unchanged.
  - *Evidence:* useReferences.js readBody l.476-488 calls downloadFile. InvoiceAttachment and BudgetView open only invoice rows, which can never be NAS rows (shape CHECK).
- **NS-X20 [minor] — taken.** Fields are read as text and checked by regex before casting, and bad entries are dropped. Claims are computed once in a CTE and anti-joined.
  - *Evidence:* Draft step 6: the jsonb_to_recordset typed casts.

#### Decisions

- **NS-D1** Office-server files are files rows with storage_provider 'network_share', a new enum value added in its own migration. The workspace vocabulary (petal, network, s3; 0051) is unchanged.
  - *Why:* Every Moth, Files, trash and move path targets files rows. 'network' would collide with the workspace axis (storage/index.js header). 'local_server' means the body is on the computer that added it.
- **NS-D2** Locator: storage_path = 'nas:' + path relative to workspace_storage.root_path, using '/' separators. A space's folder (network_share_space_folder) is (a) for a personal space, '_WILSON_PERSONAL/<personal_owner_id>'; (b) otherwise projects.folder_root when it sits strictly inside the root (0049's comparison), converted to '/'; (c) otherwise NULL, which means not listed. Invariant: a live NAS row's locator lies under its own space's folder.
  - *Why:* folder_root is written only by admins and managers (0049). The slug and title are member-writable (NS-SEC-01). The uuid folder name says nothing about who owns it. Answer 5's 'file manager' rule keeps the file where its row says.
- **NS-D3** Writer binding. GUC wilson.network_share_writer = 'check:<project uuid>' during an apply's bulk statements, or 'op:<file uuid>' around each row write of a file operation, reset to '' before return. fn_files_network_share_guard (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE, not definer) refuses with 42501 unless the GUC is bound to that row: an INSERT of network_share; a change of provider to or from network_share; and, on a NAS row, a change of storage_path, size_bytes, source_modified_at, mime_type, thumbnail_url, share_missing_since, share_bin_path, deleted_at, deleted_by, project_id, workspace_id or folder_id. The one exception is folder_id becoming NULL with nothing else changed (RI SET NULL). Names, descriptions, tags and entity links stay on files_update.
  - *Why:* A GUC test, not current_user, so other definers (soft_delete_row, moth_move_items, restore_soft_deleted) cannot change a NAS row without moving the real file. It composes with the bodies area's fn_files_body_guard.
- **NS-D4** Identity: UNIQUE (workspace_id, lower(storage_path)) WHERE storage_provider='network_share' AND deleted_at IS NULL. A trashed row keeps its original storage_path, and its bin location is in share_bin_path. Restore refuses 'occupied' when a live row holds that locator. Rename match: a vanished live row in a complete directory of this call pairs with an unclaimed entry of the same lower(leaf), size and mtime, unique on both sides.
  - *Why:* Ids survive checks, and a new file at a deleted file's old name can be listed. Locators held by pending operations are excluded (NS-X04).
- **NS-D5** Missing marking: share_missing_since = now() on this project's live NAS rows that sit directly in a directory the call marks complete and are not seen, or that sit under a subfolder missing from a complete directory's subdirs. A row with a pending operation is never marked. A seen row is cleared. A directory refused with EACCES or EPERM, or cut by a limit or the deadline, is sent as incomplete.
  - *Why:* Correct across calls (NS-X05) and partly honest about access the checker lacks (NS-SEC-05). Nothing is deleted by a check.
- **NS-D6** public.network_share_checks holds one row per space (project_id PK). It records last checked (clock_timestamp), who checked, whether the check was complete, counts, next_start (rotation) and a rate window. Only the apply writes it. Failures stay on that computer.
  - *Why:* Keeps projects free of churn, broadcasts and history noise. One laptop off the VPN does not tell the company the server is down.
- **NS-D7** Desktop walk: electron/networkShare.cjs. The route takes the space folder as a locator, never an absolute path. Steps: same-origin + token; UNC root; checkShareLocator; rootReachable; then the worker_threads Worker (eval precedent; refusal lists and the pure sequence classifier passed in) walks with lstat, never following links or junctions, and refuses unless the folder's realpath is inside the root's realpath. Per call: 5,000 entries, 2,000 directories, depth 8, 60 s. Excluded at any depth: rabbit_money_segment names; *_RECEIPTS&INVOICES, *_CREWINVOICES, *_TALENTINVOICES and *_DATABASES; dot-segments (so '.wilson-bin' is read only as the bin list at the space's top); node_modules, desktop.ini and Thumbs.db. The walk records metadata only.
  - *Why:* A sync SMB walk froze the window (bins, MEASURED). A '_DATABASES' folder holds money JSON (mirrorProjectDatabases). Sequences would break the cap (NS-X07).
- **NS-D8** Claims and nesting. network_share_claims(ws) returns (project_id, prefix, placed) for every project in any state: a placed folder_root; folder_slug when folder_root is NULL and the slug is one valid segment; the personal folder; and (NULL, '_WILSON_PERSONAL'). fn_projects_network_share_folder_guard (SECURITY DEFINER, BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF folder_root, folder_slug, personal_owner_id) refuses a prefix equal to, containing or inside another claim ('this folder is already used by another project or space on the company server'). The apply refuses 'folders_unplaced' when any claim is unplaced, and 'folder_shared' when its folder overlaps another claim.
  - *Why:* Fails closed (NS-SEC-02/06/07). The sentence reveals only a folder the manager can already see on the share.
- **NS-D9** Who acts. A check (walk + apply) needs can_manage_project_roster AND passes_project_privacy, or the owner for a personal space; on the client this is canOnProject('project.roster.manage', {ready}) plus the privacy test. A file operation (add, move, delete, restore, copy-up) needs can_write_project AND passes_project_privacy, or the owner; on the client, 'project.entity.write'. Automatic checks run when a project opens in R.A.B.B.I.T. or is picked in Files, on the desktop, in cloud mode, in a byos+network+unc workspace, with no offline sign-in, and with a 2-minute cool-down. Refresh is a GatedAction.
  - *Why:* This is her NAS ruling (open plus Refresh) with the narrowest view-publishing seat, pending NS-Q1.
- **NS-D10** Realtime. The files arm of fn_realtime_broadcast returns early when the GUC equals 'check:'||the row's project_id. File operations broadcast normally. network_share_checks uses fn_network_share_checks_broadcast (0101 shape), which realtimeMerge maps to {type:'files-refetch'}.
  - *Why:* One signal per check instead of 5,000 row broadcasts. The 18-table trigger pins stay true (NS-X01).
- **NS-D11** file_events. An INSERT under 'check:' records 'catalogued'. A WILSON add records 'uploaded'. On an UPDATE that changes project_id, no 'moved' event is written (a path would name the other space's folder; the moves area's 'refiled' covers it). A purge certificate of a NAS row adds details.body = 'company server bin' when share_bin_path is set, else 'left on the company server'. file_events_event_check gains 'catalogued'.
  - *Why:* The audit trail stays true (TPN-CONT-002) and leaks no private folder.
- **NS-D12** File-manager operations (answers 1, 3, 5). Each is server-first. A definer RPC validates, writes the row's target state and journals the operation in network_share_ops ('pending'). The desktop then moves the real file (refusing to overwrite) and calls network_share_settle(op_ids, 'done'|'not_moved'). 'not_moved' replays the origin from the journal. Delete: a confirmation, then network_share_trash, then a rename into '<space>/.wilson-bin/<id>/<leaf>'. Restore: network_share_restore, then the rename back. Move: network_share_move to the destination space's folder + folders.path + leaf. Undo: network_share_undo(op_id) replays the journal. Add: a desktop copy, then network_share_record_added. Copy-up: network_share_adopt_local turns a local_server row into network_share at its destination; the copy is verified before settling. Purge for good: the apply returns the bin ids holding a 'purged' certificate in this project, and the desktop removes them. pending_mine lets the person who started an operation finish it later. Pending operations whose locators are both inside this folder are handed to any check-seat holder after 24 h. In a browser every NAS operation says it is done from the desktop app.
  - *Why:* Her answer 5 verbatim. The journal makes crashes recoverable without duplicates and without trusting caller paths.
- **NS-D13** Opening and reading. GET/HEAD /api/rabbit/network-share/file/<locator> and the 'share' source of rabbit:open-path run: same-origin + token, checkShareLocator, rootReachable, shareContain (async), then sendFile with ranges, safeMediaContentType and nosniff. networkShareProvider.js implements all six registry functions: get, exists and getUrl via the route; put and del via the op routes, on the desktop only. Off the desktop each throws 'Preview available on the desktop app' (for reads) or 'Do this from the WILSON desktop app' (for writes).
  - *Why:* The registry contract holds. The person's own SMB rights stay the authority over file bodies.
- **NS-D14** Catalogue rows never authorize the gateway. The post-condition requires that gateway_clips_for_tickets and gateway_sync_apply (latest 0104), with comments stripped, mention neither 'public.files' nor 'network_share'. Suite 96 gets a probe.
  - *Why:* TPN-AUTH-012.
- **NS-D15** moth_local_scopes is not built in Session 1 (answer 6). It is deferred to Session 2 with these constraints: no cloud backing project in projects_select, the projects index or channels; no local id in the cloud; the decision waits for NS-Q2.
  - *Why:* No Session 1 feature writes Moth state for a local item. A backing project would leak through 0072.
- **NS-D16** Desktop-only projects. Every Moth node is keyed (scope 'cloud'|'local', id). In Session 1 they are read-only through the active adapter. In Local Server mode a project is labelled 'On this computer only' and has no live features. A local project listed in bundle.cloudMigration[ws].carried is hidden beside its cloud copy. Nothing local reaches a cloud table, channel or count. Moves between two local projects move the real file (answer 2) and belong to the moves area.
  - *Why:* It cannot impersonate a cloud identity, and no placeholder reveals that a local project exists.
- **NS-D17** Withdrawn. Local Moth state (S2-S4) is not designed here. Recorded constraint: never a field of bundle.project (the _DATABASES mirror), and only if the owner allows it (NS-Q2).
  - *Why:* NS-X09, NS-SEC-12.
- **NS-Q1** QUESTION FOR AUDREY. 'When WILSON checks a project's folder on your office server, it lists everything the person checking can open there, and everyone on the project then sees those file names. If your server hides some folders from some people, which do you want? (a) Only company admins and managers, and each project's managers, run the check, and the Storage page says the list shows what they can open (recommended). (b) Anyone who can edit the project runs it. (c) WILSON lists only the folders it made itself.' The build uses (a) until she answers.
  - *Why:* NS-SEC-05/NS-X08. Server permissions cannot be read from file details.
- **NS-Q2** QUESTION FOR AUDREY (before Session 2). 'For a project kept only on one computer, may Moth keep its layout, tags and boards on that computer, or should they wait until the project moves to the cloud?' Session 1 needs no answer.
  - *Why:* Her rule that database data lives in Supabase (rules item 43).

#### Schema changes

- **TYPE public.storage_provider** (restates from: new (enum 0000, last widened 0051)): NS-1, its own migration: ALTER TYPE public.storage_provider ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS 'network_share'; plus a pg_enum post-condition. Nothing uses the value in that transaction.
- **FUNCTIONS network_share_locator_ok(text), network_share_bin_ok(text)** (restates from: new): IMMUTABLE sql, search_path pinned. locator_ok is as in the draft: not empty, length <= 1024, NFC; no leading or trailing '/', '//', '\\', ':' or *?"<>|; no C0/C1, bidi, zero-width, BOM, variation-selector or TAG code point; no dot-led segment; no segment ending in '.' or ' '; no rabbit_money_segment segment; no segment matching _(receipts&invoices|crewinvoices|talentinvoices|databases)$. bin_ok: p ~ '^(.+)/\.wilson-bin/([0-9a-f-]{36})/([^/]+)$', and both captured parts pass locator_ok. REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC, anon.
- **FUNCTIONS network_share_space_folder(uuid), network_share_claims(uuid)** (restates from: new; personal_owner_id from the personal-space area's migration): SECURITY DEFINER STABLE, search_path pinned, REVOKE from PUBLIC, anon and authenticated (definer callers only). space_folder: NULL unless workspace_storage has mode 'byos', provider 'network' and root_kind 'unc'. For a personal space it returns '_WILSON_PERSONAL/'||personal_owner_id; otherwise folder_root placed by 0049's test (lower(left(fr,len+1)) = lower(root)||'\\'), converted with replace('\\','/'), and NULL unless locator_ok. claims: RETURNS TABLE(project_id uuid, prefix text, placed boolean), as in NS-D8, every project in the workspace in any deleted state.
- **projects: fn_projects_network_share_folder_guard + trg_projects_network_share_folder_guard; CHECK projects_folder_slug_shape_chk** (restates from: new): BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF folder_root, folder_slug, personal_owner_id, SECURITY DEFINER, service_role passes. Refuses a claim prefix that overlaps another claim in the same workspace (NS-D8). Refuses an UPDATE of a non-NULL folder_slug without the COALESCE'd admin/manager seat. CHECK (folder_slug IS NULL OR (folder_slug !~ '[/\\]' AND network_share_locator_ok(folder_slug))), added NOT VALID and validated after a pre-flight count of zero offenders (if not zero, it stays NOT VALID and a NOTICE is raised). 0049's function is untouched.
- **COLUMNS public.files.share_missing_since timestamptz, public.files.share_bin_path text** (restates from: new): Both nullable and written only by the network_share_* RPCs. Neither is added to FILE_COLUMNS. No files_* policy is restated.
- **CONSTRAINTS files_network_share_shape_chk, files_network_share_namespace_chk, files_network_share_extra_chk** (restates from: new): The pre-flight requires zero rows with storage_path LIKE 'nas:%'. shape: provider <> 'network_share' OR (left(path,4)='nas:' AND locator_ok(substr(path,5)) AND thumbnail_url IS NULL AND NOT COALESCE(is_financial,false) AND NOT ('legal' = ANY(COALESCE(tags,'{}')))). namespace: (provider='network_share') = (left(path,4)='nas:'). extra: (share_missing_since IS NULL AND share_bin_path IS NULL) OR (provider='network_share' AND (share_bin_path IS NULL OR network_share_bin_ok(share_bin_path))). No existing files CHECK enumerates providers (0050 money_provider is 'supabase OR NOT money').
- **INDEXES on public.files** (restates from: new): UNIQUE files_network_share_locator_key ON files (workspace_id, lower(storage_path)) WHERE storage_provider='network_share' AND deleted_at IS NULL; files_network_share_project_idx ON files (project_id, lower(storage_path)) WHERE storage_provider='network_share'.
- **fn_files_network_share_guard + trg_files_network_share_guard** (restates from: new): As NS-D3. The GUC test is COALESCE(current_setting('wilson.network_share_writer',true) IN ('check:'||OLD/NEW.project_id, 'op:'||NEW.id), false). REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated.
- **TABLE public.network_share_checks** (restates from: new): project_id uuid PK REFERENCES projects ON DELETE CASCADE; workspace_id NOT NULL; folder_locator (locator_ok); last_checked_at; last_checked_by; complete bool; dirs_checked, files_seen, missing_count int >= 0; next_start text NULL (locator_ok); window_started_at timestamptz; calls_in_window int; audit columns. Triggers: populate_workspace (0004), fn_audit_touch (0001), trg_network_share_checks_broadcast. ENABLE + FORCE RLS. One policy, network_share_checks_select, with the workspace + has_active_membership + EXISTS(projects) live-parent hop, so 0072 privacy follows. Grants: SELECT to authenticated, ALL to service_role, nothing to anon.
- **TABLE public.network_share_ops (journal)** (restates from: new): id uuid PK; workspace_id; file_id REFERENCES files ON DELETE CASCADE; project_id; dest_project_id NULL; op CHECK IN ('trash','restore','move','add','adopt'); from_locator text NULL; to_locator text; origin jsonb (project_id, folder_id, links, provider, path), CHECK octet_length <= 4000; state CHECK IN ('pending','done','reverted'); created_by; created_at; settled_at. UNIQUE (file_id) WHERE state='pending'; indexes on lower(from_locator) and lower(to_locator) WHERE pending, and on (created_by) WHERE pending. ENABLE + FORCE RLS, no policies, no grants to client roles (only definers read or write it; postgres bypasses RLS). purge_network_share_ops() removes settled rows older than 90 days and is scheduled through a guarded pg_cron job, 'wilson-purge-network-share-ops', at 04:50.
- **FUNCTION network_share_catalogue_apply(p_project_id uuid, p_root text, p_folder text, p_dirs jsonb, p_bin jsonb, p_final boolean, p_stopped_at text) RETURNS jsonb** (restates from: new): SECURITY DEFINER, search_path pinned; REVOKE PUBLIC and anon; GRANT authenticated. (1) Authority (NS-D9 check seat), raising one message 'network_share: not allowed' (42501) for every failure. (2) Drive must be byos/network/unc and equal to btrim(p_root) case-folded, else 'drive_changed'. (3) Folder: space_folder equals p_folder, else 'no_folder'; any unplaced claim gives 'folders_unplaced'; an overlap gives 'folder_shared'. (4) Limits: dirs <= 2,000, files <= 5,000, bin <= 500; at most 60 calls per 10 minutes ('too_soon'); at most 50,000 rows ('too_many'). (5) pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('network_share:'||id,0)), then the GUC := 'check:'||id. (6) Parse with CTEs as text, validated before casting (s ^\d{1,15}$; m an ISO pattern between 1980 and now()+1 day; t a mime regex). Each directory must equal the folder or lie under it. Entries named by a pending operation, or held by another live row, are dropped. (7) Updates, (8) rename match, (9) inserts (name = the leaf with a Local Server '<uuid>-' prefix stripped; kind 'source'; folder_id by (project_id, lower(path)); ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING), (10) missing marks as in NS-D5. (11) Upsert the check row. (12) From the bin list: purge_ok = ids with a file_events 'purged' certificate in this project; restore_back = live rows of this project, with no pending operation, whose id is in the bin; stale = pending operations older than 24 h with both locators inside this folder. (13) GUC := ''. Returns {added, updated, renamed, found_again, newly_missing, purge_ok, restore_back, stale, checked_at}.
- **FUNCTIONS network_share_record_added, network_share_trash, network_share_restore, network_share_move, network_share_adopt_local, network_share_settle, network_share_undo, network_share_pending_mine** (restates from: new): All SECURITY DEFINER, search_path pinned, REVOKE PUBLIC and anon, GRANT authenticated. Each validates every row before writing and binds 'op:'||id for each row write. record_added(p_project, p_root, p_folder_id, p_entries <= 50): the file-op seat, then entries at space_folder + folders.path; event 'uploaded'. trash(p_ids <= 500): the file-op seat; NAS row, live, no pending operation; soft_delete_row('files',id) under the GUC (fn_trash_authz still runs); share_bin_path set; journal 'trash'. restore(p_ids): refuses 'occupied'; restore_soft_deleted; journal 'restore'. move(p_ids <= 500, p_dest_project, p_dest_folder): both seats; the destination folder belongs to the destination project and is not locked; target = destination space_folder/path/leaf; refuses 'occupied' and 'no_folder'. A cross-project move clears entity links and is_core_definer (the moves area's rule) and sets the moves area's move GUC. Journal 'move' with origin. adopt_local(p_id, p_dest_project, p_dest_folder): a local_server row becomes network_share at its target; journal 'adopt' with the origin path. settle(p_op_ids, p_outcome, p_facts jsonb): only the creator, or a check-seat holder for stale in-folder operations. 'done' closes the operation (restore clears share_bin_path; adopt takes size and mtime from p_facts, validated). 'not_moved' replays the origin. undo(p_op_id): a done move or adopt, replayed from origin into a new operation. pending_mine(): the caller's pending operations whose rows they can still write, as {op_id, op, from, to}. Every return value carries ids and the caller's own locators only.
- **fn_realtime_broadcast; fn_network_share_checks_broadcast** (restates from: 0098_table_views.sql (or the latest Session 1 restatement)): Restate the latest body (0098, or the Session 1 migration that last restated it) and add one line at the head of the files arm: IF current_setting('wilson.network_share_writer',true) = 'check:'||(v_row->>'project_id') THEN RETURN NULL; END IF;. fn_network_share_checks_broadcast is 0101's shape: realtime.send of {id, project_id} to rabbit:project:<id>, a catalog probe, a catch-all WARNING, and REVOKE from all client roles.
- **fn_file_events_capture; file_events_event_check** (restates from: 0074_file_events_money_arm.sql / 0093_file_gateway.sql (or the moves area's restatement)): Restate the latest body (0074, or the moves area's restatement) with NS-D11's three additions. The 0093 DO-block drops the CHECK and adds it back with the full list ('uploaded','downloaded','moved','relinked','trashed','restored','purged','upload_abandoned','viewed_remote', plus 'refiled' when the moves area's restatement is present, plus 'catalogued'). The post-condition reads every term back.
- **Pre-flight and post-conditions of NS-2 (load-bearing; function text compared with comments stripped)** (restates from: new): Pre-flight: the enum label exists; projects.personal_owner_id exists; edit_history_select carries the repairs privacy arm; the restated bodies match their recorded md5s; there are zero 'nas:%' paths. Post-conditions: the three files CHECKs and the unique index exist; both guards are attached; both tables have RLS enabled and forced, with checks holding exactly one SELECT policy and ops holding none; authenticated has no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on checks and no privilege at all on ops; every new definer has prosecdef, a pinned search_path and no anon EXECUTE; 'wilson.network_share_writer' appears only in the network_share_* bodies, the guard, fn_realtime_broadcast and fn_file_events_capture, and every RPC body contains the reset to ''; the gateway text test (NS-D14); trg_files_gc_enqueue exists; probes of locator_ok and bin_ok (in the 0091 style) refuse 'a/INVOICES/x', 'a/P_DATABASES/b', 'a/.wilson/x', 'a/../b', 'a\\b', 'C:/x', U+202E and non-NFC text, and admit 'Salt-Hours/rushes/A001.mov'.

Reword SERVER_MEDIA_SENTENCE: files in each project's folder on the company server are listed here, and are opened, added, moved and deleted in the desktop app. Fix the stale getStorageProvider(local_server) comment. NEW_BODY_GOES_TO is unchanged.
- **src/tools/rabbit_v0.1.0/adapters/supabaseAdapter.js**: Register the network_share provider. New methods: refreshNetworkShare (one apply per batch), getNetworkShareCheck (42P01 gives null), and networkShareOp(kind, args) for the eight RPCs, mapping each refusal code to a sentence. deleteFile, restoreFile and moveFiles route NAS rows to the NAS flow, or throw DESKTOP_ACTION_SENTENCE off the desktop. FILE_COLUMNS leaves out share_missing_since and share_bin_path.

The banner reads 'Listed from the company's server · last checked <time> by <name>', notes a partial check and offers Refresh (GatedAction, roster seat) or 'Refresh from the desktop app'. The delete confirmation says WILSON moves the file to the server's bin, that it can be undone or restored from Trash for 30 days, and that it is then removed for good. In a personal space of a network workspace, the add flow says: 'These files are kept on your company's server. WILSON shows them only to you, but anyone who opens that server folder directly can find them.'

- **Admin Storage (StorageSection)**: Before a root change it warns with the count of listed files. It lists projects whose folder is outside the drive or overlaps another (admins only). It says the list shows what the person checking can open.
