- **.github/workflows/rls.yml: failure-replay list**: Append NNN_moth_history.sql in number order.
- **WILSON/src/tools/rabbit_v0.1.0/storage/rlsSuiteLists.test.js: listed.slice(-5) and picked.slice(-6) pins and their dated comments**: Move both tails so they end with the new suite (the integrator applies every S1 suite at once).
- **WILSON/supabase/migrations/NNNN_moth_history.sql (new; the last S1 migration, numbered at the tip, at or after 0107)**: Everything in schema_changes for Session 1. No ALTER TYPE, so it runs as one transaction.
- **WILSON/supabase/tests/rls/NNN_moth_history.sql (new)**: The suite in tests[0].
- **WILSON/docs/SYSTEMS_HANDBOOK.md: §4.1 FORCE exceptions, §4.8 retention and cron, §13.3 edit history, §14 arrows that do not exist**: moth_history goes in the not-forced list, and wilson-purge-moth-history at 04:45 with 90 days. Describe it beside edit_history: the different audience, the privacy, personal and money arms, redact-on-lock and RPC-only reads. Record that it is on no realtime topic and in no takeout. Same commit as the migration.
- **WILSON/src/components/AdminTerminal/WorkspaceTakeout.jsx header comment**: One line: moth_history is excluded (derived, RPC-only, carries personal-space metadata). TAKEOUT_TABLES is unchanged.
- **WILSON/docs/sessions/PLAN_MOTH.md §4 and §5 (controller's record)**: Apply answer 6. S1 scope: the history record only, in this area. S2 Database line: 'S2 creates view/layout; S1 provides history'. §4 suite numbers are assigned at creation, not 110 to 123. The RLS_TABLES target count is recomputed, and moth_local_scopes and file_body_locations are dropped from §4.
- **Later sessions' restatements**: S2: restate _moth_target_eligible (kinds). S3 and S4: restate fn_moth_history_capture, moth_history_project and moth_history_projection_ok from their latest bodies (md5 guard), widen the entity CHECK by NOT VALID then VALIDATE in a CHECK-only migration, and write baselines. fn_realtime_broadcast and fn_trash_authz are restated from whatever is latest at their tip.

#### Client changes

- **Session 1: none**: Database side only. No client export, per plan step 9: no feature exports before the UI that consumes them. *(production caller: n/a)*
- **Session 2: src/tools/rabbit_v0.1.0/adapters/supabaseAdapter.js (fetchMothHistory, fetchMothHistoryWindow), the dev fixtures adapter twin, and localServerAdapter.js**: Thin wrappers over moth_history_read and moth_history_window, with the absent-function probe: 42883 or PGRST202 gives a 'history unavailable' state. The Local Server adapter returns no rows and recording_since = now() for every type. Rebuild rule: start from the live authorised graph and undo rows newest-first. 'added' means absent before; 'removed' means present before with the prior projection; an entity is shown at T only if its ancestors are. Everything before recording_since, for that type and scope, is labelled not recorded. Unknown ids are ignored. *(production caller: Session 2 HUD history scrub)*
- **Sessions 2 to 4: adapters for views, positions, tags, links, notes, boards and locks**: Created with their tables and their consuming UI in the same session. *(production caller: S2 view widget and drag-settle; S3 tag, link and note UI; S4 board window)*

#### Migration order

- **S1-a (other areas, first)**: Privacy repairs (write hops, money and history arm, workspace channel). Personal space (personal_owner_id, identity guard, ensure_personal_space, _moth_target_eligible and moth_can_read/write_target, refactored onto moth_file_row_locked and moth_folder_path_locked; folder lock guard). Moves (composite FKs, move guards, moth_move_items, file_moves). Bodies (fn_files_body_guard, rekey). NAS (the enum value in its own migration, then the catalogue and share_missing_since).
  - *Constraints:* Each restates guard bodies from the latest migration and lands with its own suite and RLS_TABLES line. The history migration's pre-flight refuses until all of them are present.
- **S1-b: NNNN_moth_history.sql + NNN_moth_history.sql + rls.yml + rlsSuiteLists.test.js, one commit**: Pre-flight; table, CHECKs and indexes; projector and checker; RLS and revokes; capture and the 22 triggers; baseline marks; reader and window; purge and cron; post-conditions.
  - *Constraints:* Rehearse rolled back on wilson-dev (collected = planned). Apply file by file with the schema_migrations md5. Run the whole pgTAP set before push, including 01 to 05, 17, 33, 35, 48 to 52, 78, 82 and 99. CREATE TRIGGER briefly takes SHARE ROW EXCLUSIVE on 11 hot tables, so apply off-hours on staging and production, on her go.
- **S2, S3, S4**: Per the deferred specs. Any widening of moth_history's CHECK is a migration that does only that (DROP + ADD NOT VALID, lock_timeout 5s), followed by a VALIDATE migration.
  - *Constraints:* Remeasure numbers at each tip. Each new table's suite and RLS_TABLES line come before anything depends on it.

#### Tests

- **supabase/tests/rls/NNN_moth_history.sql (Session 1)**: STRUCTURE:
- RLS on, FORCE off; zero policies; no table or sequence privilege for anon or authenticated; new definers not anon-executable and search_path pinned; the read result type has no bigint; the WHEN text is pinned by pg_get_triggerdef.
CAPTURE:
- A shared-project file insert writes one 'create', and its state keys are within the allowlist.
- A rename writes 'update'. A folder_id change writes 'move'. A project_id-only move made through the moves RPC writes 'move'.
- soft_delete_row writes 'trash'; restore_soft_deleted writes 'restore'.
- An is_private flip writes a row whose state_class is 'private'.
- A phase insert, rename and delete each write a row (0119 as built: a child of a project that has gone writes none, because no side of it can pass the gate).
- A folder label change carries the name in prior and state.
- A task_items create has item_type and item_id.
- A 5 KB title writes a row with truncated set.
- A description-only update writes nothing.
- A private project with created_by NULL is captured as class private with owner NULL.
- A NAS catalogue insert (with the writer setting on) is captured, and share_missing_since gives missing=true.
MONEY:
- An INVOICES-path file, an is_financial file and a 'legal'-tagged file each write no row.
- Create, rename, then flag is_financial: as postgres, every row of F is locked with NULL projections; an admin and a member read 0 rows.
- After a trash and a purge_soft_deleted run, still 0 rows.
- A file named by expenses.file_ids, linked without any files write, is hidden from an admin by the live gate.
- Renaming the INVOICES folder to FILES is refused by the lock guard.
CHECKS:
- One fixture breaks each of the six CHECKs, by its name.
READER:
- A workspace B user naming A's project, an inactive member, a random uuid and another person's personal space all get the same refusal.
- A member and a reviewer read a shared project.
- A private project is refused to a non-creator member and to a manager, and is readable by its creator and an admin.
- A personal space is readable by its owner and refused to an admin, in both scoped and aggregate reads.
AUDIENCE:
- A file moved from private X (creator C) into shared P, after which C flips X to shared: member M and manager N each get one 'added' row with prior and prior_project_id NULL.
- A file moved from P into private Y: M gets no row for that file, the create included.
WINDOW:
- A baseline gives GREATEST(mark, created_at, now() - 90 days); a workspace with no mark gives its created_at.
- Narrowing the entity CHECK (folders dropped) inside the transaction: a folder insert still succeeds, and a 'capture_failed' mark scoped to P exists. The window gives P's folders row at that time. A restart inside U's personal space is invisible to an admin and to another member.
PURGE:
- A row at now() - 91 days is deleted, and one at now() - 89 days is kept.
- A retention under 1 day raises.
- authenticated EXECUTE on the purge gives 42501.
TEARDOWN:
- Deleting a workspace that has projects and files succeeds and leaves no rows.
PLANTED CONTROLS (each must turn its named probe red):
(1) The present-audience call replaced by passes_project_privacy → the admin personal-space probes.
(2) The private class arm returns true → M's 'added with prior NULL'.
(3) A manager arm in the private class → N's probe.
(4) The entity live gate dropped → the moved-into-Y probe.
(5) The redaction dropped → the locked-rows probe.
(6) The live money gate dropped → the expense-linked probe.
(7) storage_path added to the allowlist → the CHECK probe.
(8) The phases join dropped → the phase probe.
(9) A skip on projection equality alone → the trash probe.
(10) The capture_failed insert removed → the restart probe.
(11) The window's side test removed → the personal restart probe.
(12) The purge comparison changed to <= 89 days → the 89-day keep probe.
- **Existing suites 01_projects, 02_phases, 03_assets, 04_tasks, 05_files, 17 edit_history, 33_file_lifecycle, 35_platform_audit, 48 to 51, 52_folders, 78_file_events_money, 82_private_project_definers, 99_task_items**: Unchanged and green: the new AFTER triggers change no write outcome. 35_platform_audit's anon-executable SECURITY DEFINER count catches any missed REVOKE (planted: grant anon on moth_history_read and it goes red). A full tap-all run with collected = planned.
- **S2 to S4 suites (deferred)**: Per table: tenant, inactive, owner, admin, manager and reviewer. An admin gets 0 rows into another person's personal space, through positions, refs, relationships, assignments and board elements. A member gets no ref or element into private X. An admin gets none after a file is flagged. A board under LEGAL is refused. Cross-tenant moth_can_write_target is FALSE, the same as for a random id. Composite FKs refuse cross-workspace ids with the validate trigger's code. Planted: each hop swapped for plain RLS, and the composite FK dropped.

#### Risks

- A structural write that bypasses the triggers (session_replication_role=replica, a COPY, or a WHEN list missing a column) silently corrupts the backwards rebuild before it.
  - *Mitigation:* The triggers fire for every role, service_role included. The WHEN text is pinned in the post-condition and in the suite. Any migration that suspends capture must write a 'suspended' mark (handbook rule). A failed capture writes a scoped restart. The client degrades to showing the current state.
- Write cost on hot tables and bursts on cascades or NAS first checks (up to 5,000 rows).
  - *Mitigation:* WHEN-filtered UPDATE triggers write one row per change. The volume matches what edit_history already absorbs. Redaction touches only the rows of an entity that has just become locked. S1 records row counts on wilson-dev, and S2 measures reader latency.
- Redaction depends on the classifier, and drift between capture and Moth's live gate could leave unredacted names.
  - *Mitigation:* One helper, moth_file_row_locked, is called by both _moth_target_eligible and the capture. The folder lock guard makes path trustworthy. The live gate stays as a second defence (the expense-link probe). The bodies area guarantees money-ness only grows.
- Restating the capture, projector or CHECK in S3 or S4 could drop tables, or stall writes behind ACCESS EXCLUSIVE.
  - *Mitigation:* An md5 pre-flight and a re-run refusal. The both-ways trigger and CHECK post-condition is re-asserted. CHECK widening is NOT VALID, then VALIDATE, in its own migrations with lock_timeout.
- Admin JWT lag: a demoted admin keeps reading private-project history for up to an hour.
  - *Mitigation:* This is the same rule as 0072 projects_select (rule 20 allows claims for reads). The personal arm uses auth.uid() only.
- Late-committing transactions can fall behind the reader's cursor, because observed_at is the transaction start.
  - *Mitigation:* Documented. The scrub re-reads the first page when it opens, and rows de-duplicate by row_key.
- Cross-area names may land differently (move RPC: moth_move_items vs move_files; file_events: refiled vs moved_out/moved_in; the classifier helper names).
  - *Mitigation:* Capture is writer-agnostic: it detects project_id and folder_id changes whatever the writer. The pre-flight names exact dependencies, so a rename is a substitution. The integrator settles names in the interfaces list.

#### Interfaces with the other areas

- **moth_history_read** (provides): public.moth_history_read(p_scope_project uuid DEFAULT NULL, p_since timestamptz DEFAULT NULL, p_after uuid DEFAULT NULL, p_limit int DEFAULT 1000) RETURNS TABLE(row_key uuid, entity_type text, entity_id uuid, action text, observed_at timestamptz, actor_user_id uuid, prior_project_id uuid, prior jsonb, state_project_id uuid, state jsonb). Newest first. action is one of added, changed, moved, trashed, restored, removed. Single refusal: 42501 'that history is not available'. GRANT authenticated.
- **moth_history_window** (provides): public.moth_history_window(p_scope_project uuid DEFAULT NULL) RETURNS TABLE(entity_type text, project_id uuid, recording_since timestamptz). A NULL project_id row is workspace-wide; a project row is a visible restart. GRANT authenticated.
- **history capture contract** (provides): fn_moth_history_capture on projects, phases, assets, tasks, files, folders, scenes, shots, levels, experiences and task_items, through trg_<t>_moth_history (AFTER INSERT OR DELETE) and trg_<t>_moth_history_upd (AFTER UPDATE WHEN the pinned columns change). It captures every writer and role, ignores wilson.checked_move and wilson.network_share_writer, and never fails the user's write. Writers should change project_id and folder_id in one UPDATE, so one 'move' row is written. A migration that suspends triggers must INSERT INTO public.moth_history(workspace_id, entity_type, entity_id, action, mark_kind) VALUES (ws, t, ws, 'mark_recording', 'suspended') per affected type.
- **history vocabulary and projection** (provides): Stored actions: create, update, move, trash, restore, delete, mark_recording; mark_kind: baseline, suspended, capture_failed; classes: shared, private, personal. Projection allowlist and per-table maps as in schema_changes. Never in the projection: storage_path, storage_provider, thumbnail, description, notes, path, money columns, person ids. moth_history reads nothing from file_events, and none of the file_events values (catalogued, refiled, moved_out/moved_in, trashed and others) touch it.
- **purge_moth_history + cron** (provides): public.purge_moth_history(p_retention interval DEFAULT '90 days') RETURNS bigint, service_role only; job 'wilson-purge-moth-history' at '45 4 * * *'. Other areas' new jobs must avoid 04:39, 04:43, 04:45, 04:47, 04:51, 04:55 and 04:59.
- **personal-space classifier columns** (needs): From the personal-space area: projects.personal_owner_id uuid NULL with projects_personal_shape_chk (personal ⇒ is_private AND created_by = personal_owner_id), and fn_projects_identity_guard, which pins created_by and makes personal_owner_id immutable. Class derivation: personal_owner_id NOT NULL → personal (owner = personal_owner_id); is_private → private (owner = created_by); otherwise shared.
- **Moth project/target gate** (needs): From the personal-space area: public._moth_target_eligible(p_kind text, p_id uuid, p_allow_trashed boolean) RETURNS boolean, STABLE SECURITY DEFINER, caller-relative (workspace, live membership, live project in workspace, passes_project_privacy, personal owner-only, money excluded), executable by the definer owner; and moth_can_read_target / moth_can_write_target(p_kind text, p_id uuid), GRANT authenticated, for the S2 to S4 policies. S2 widens the kinds.
- **row-image money classifier** (needs): From the personal-space area: public.moth_file_row_locked(p_workspace uuid, p_project uuid, p_file uuid, p_flag boolean, p_path text, p_tags text[], p_folder uuid) RETURNS boolean, STABLE SECURITY DEFINER, search_path pinned, REVOKEd from PUBLIC, anon and authenticated. It is file_row_is_money(p_flag, p_path) OR 'legal' = ANY(p_tags) OR the folder's path is locked OR an expenses row in p_workspace has file_ids @> ARRAY[p_file]. The file arm of _moth_target_eligible must call it. Also public.moth_folder_path_locked(p_path text) RETURNS boolean, IMMUTABLE: rabbit_money_segment(split_part(coalesce(p_path,''),'/',1)).
- **folder lock guard** (needs): From the personal-space and classifier area (the integrator assigns an owner): fn_folders_lock_guard() with trg_folders_lock_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF path, slug, parent_id ON public.folders. RAISE 42501 when moth_folder_path_locked(OLD.path) IS DISTINCT FROM moth_folder_path_locked(NEW.path) on UPDATE, or when a non-root parent's first path segment differs from NEW's. The pre-flight counts existing drift.
- **move operation** (needs): From the moves area: the cloud move (moth_move_items(file_ids uuid[], folder_ids uuid[], dest_project uuid, dest_folder uuid); the bodies area calls it move_files, and the integrator picks one) changes files.project_id and folder_id only inside the transaction-local wilson.checked_move setting, and clears the seven entity links and is_core_definer on a cross-project move. It needs the composite FKs files(folder_id, project_id) → folders(id, project_id) and folders(parent_id, project_id) → folders(id, project_id). moth_undo_move replays through the same path. History captures each as 'move'.
- **files column guard and trusted writers** (needs): From the bodies area: fn_files_body_guard refuses client changes to project_id, workspace_id, storage_provider, storage_path, thumbnail_url, is_financial and folder_id. The trusted writers are the move RPC, rekey_file_body (which may set is_financial: money-ness only grows) and network_share_catalogue_apply (wilson.network_share_writer). Capture writes only moth_history and needs no bypass.
- **body provider values** (needs): storage_provider enum: supabase, google_drive, local_server, s3, plus network_share (NAS area, in its own migration). It is never projected. For answer 1, the copy-up of a private local body is a rekey plus a move: the rekey row is skipped as non-structural and the move is captured.
- **NAS catalogue columns** (needs): From the NAS area: files.share_missing_since timestamptz NULL, written only by network_share_catalogue_apply. It must exist before the history migration; if the NAS migration lands later, it restates trg_files_moth_history_upd and moth_history_project to add it.
- **workspace-channel suppression** (provides): moth_history has no realtime trigger and is in no arm of fn_realtime_broadcast or fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast (the repairs area's A-R1-04 restatement must not add it). History is pull-only through the RPCs.

### 2.6 The privacy repairs

Findings: 4 partly, 17 taken.

**Scope as resolved:** Privacy repairs (Session 1, before any Moth table): write-side project hops on every clause, the projects owner/visibility guard, same-project references, the money gate's and edit history's privacy arm, private/personal suppression on the workspace channel, the per-project topic split, and a topic epoch so a project made private after the fact stops reaching already-joined subscribers.

**Summary:** Revised from the draft after reading the code for all 21 review findings. 19 are taken in full or in part, and 2 are taken in part with one option refuted (SEC-10 stays a recorded residual; PR-10's 'INDEX' ping is refuted). Changes from the draft:
(1) Edit history now attaches each row to the project it was in when the change happened. A row whose change crosses projects, or whose dependency endpoints sit in different projects, is stamped NULL, which leaves it readable by admins only. A new edit_history.captured_private column remembers whether the project was private at the time, so private-era rows stay readable only by the creator and admins. The backfill rebuilds the 'project at time t' from history instead of the live row (SEC-1).
(2) One projects guard. It pins created_by, lets only the creator or an admin change is_private, refuses a producer change from anyone who cannot manage the roster (closing the seat-minting escalation, SEC-6), and bumps a new projects.topic_epoch whenever a project's audience narrows. The personal-space area restates this guard rather than adding a second one (SEC-2, PR-04).
(3) The per-project topic gains the epoch suffix and a per-side split, so a row moved out of a private project never carries private values to the public topic, and a subscriber who joined before a flip hears nothing afterwards (SEC-4, SEC-7).
(4) Dependency updates check both endpoints, and a same-project reference guard covers secondary links, uuid arrays and dependency endpoints (SEC-3, SEC-9).
(5) The catalogue scans are rebuilt so that they apply on today's schema and catch the shapes that matter: one SELECT policy on projects, every permissive policy, JOIN-shaped hops, endpoint anchors, definer-only tables, Moth's owner-only personal arm (PR-01, PR-02, SEC-5).
(6) The workspace broadcast judges each side first and applies 0018's filters only to the side it sends (SEC-8).
(7) Suite 83 probe 20 and the md5 pins in suites 100 and 101 are added to the files to change. Client gaps are closed: a revalidate flag held in a ref, a sequence guard on index refreshes, and clearing the saved active project on close (PR-03, PR-08, PR-11).
Her six answers touch this area in four places. Answer 1: copy-up moves cross from private to shared, which the history NULL stamp and the topic split cover. Answer 4: admins must keep their RLS read path to personal spaces, so no admin arm is removed from projects_select. Answer 5: no change here. Answer 6: this area creates no table, only two columns, and Moth-table policy rules are recorded as a deferred spec.
Three migrations: 0107 (A), 0108 (B), 0109 (C), with suites 110, 111 and 112, all landing before the personal-space, moves, NAS and history migrations. The integrator must reconcile five interfaces with other areas:
- the one guard on projects (personal area);
- one restatement chain of fn_realtime_broadcast (moves and NAS restate from 0109, and the moves area drops its own files split);
- the wilson.checked_move setting name (moves);
- the move operation's name (moth_move_items in the moves area versus move_files in the bodies area);
- the file_events vocabulary ('refiled' in the moves area versus 'moved_out'/'moved_in' in the bodies area).

#### Findings and their dispositions

- **SEC-1 [blocker] — taken.** R8. The capture resolves the project of BOTH sides. When they differ (a project_id change, a child whose FK moved to another project's parent, or a comment re-pointed), or when a dependency's endpoints sit in different projects, project_id is NULL, which means admins only. fn_edit_history_money_snapshot is restated from 0092: it keeps 0092's pinned lines and adds `IF NEW.action='update' AND NEW.diff ? 'project_id' THEN NEW.project_id := NULL; END IF;`. The backfill uses edit_history_project_at (a 'project at time t' walk over the entity's or parent's own recorded changes), not the live row. It re-derives files rows only for entities with a recorded project change. Suite 111 adds a probe that a P→Q move leaves no P-era or P-naming row readable by M2, with a planted control.
  - *Evidence:* 0061 fn_edit_history_capture: on UPDATE v_row := to_jsonb(NEW), and the diff is {key:{old,new}}. 0088 edit_history_file_class: project_id := COALESCE(live f.project_id, diff…). 0092 fn_edit_history_money_snapshot is BEFORE INSERT and does SELECT c.is_money, c.project_id INTO NEW.is_financial, NEW.project_id, so the live row wins. The draft's backfill passed NULL for every update row, so the resolver read the live row.
- **SEC-2 [blocker] — partly.** R5 and R12. P1 restates fn_projects_owner_guard (the latest from 0107) to refuse, with 42501 and plain words, any change of is_private, created_by or personal_owner_id on a row whose OLD.personal_owner_id is set, admins included. P1 also restates projects_update (from 0107) with `AND (personal_owner_id IS NULL OR personal_owner_id = auth.uid())`. R13 records a rule as a deferred spec: every Moth policy or reader on a personal-capable row carries the owner-only arm with no admin leg. Suite 110 scan S6 enforces it, with a planted temp-table control. The admin arm stays in projects_select, because answer 4 (admins open personal spaces from Files) needs the RLS read.
  - *Evidence:* 0072 projects_select and 0013 can_write_project carry admin arms. The personal area's projects_personal_shape_chk CHECK (personal_owner_id IS NULL OR (is_private AND created_by = personal_owner_id)) binds every role, so once P1 lands an admin's flip of a personal space already fails (23514). In Session 1, Moth reads go through moth_can_read_target, whose personal arm is owner-only with no admin leg, and no Session 1 Moth table has client policies (moth_history and file_moves are definer-only). So the claim that 'D2 admits admins to personal Moth rows' has no target in Session 1.
- **SEC-3 [major] — taken.** R2: task_deps_update and phase_deps_update gain a WITH CHECK equal to their INSERT check (both endpoints). R6: the same-project guard refuses, for client roles, an edge whose endpoints are in different projects. R8 and R10: history stamps NULL, and the broadcast skips any side whose endpoints differ. task_deps_* leave the exception list, and scan S3 requires both anchors on INSERT and UPDATE checks.
  - *Evidence:* 0013 task_deps_update has USING on the predecessor only and no WITH CHECK. 0061 phase_deps_update has the same shape. 0000 task_dependencies has no same-project constraint. 0098 fn_realtime_broadcast routes dependency rows by predecessor_id, and the 0061 capture resolves them by predecessor_id. Inserts are already safe: 0013 task_deps_insert checks the successor under tasks_select, which hops (0097).
- **SEC-4 [major] — taken.** R10. 0109 restates fn_realtime_broadcast from 0098, keeping every arm, the files money arm first and comments through fn_comment_project_id. It resolves each side's project. When the two differ, it sends a DELETE-shaped (NULL, OLD) event to the old project's topic and an INSERT-shaped (NEW, NULL) event to the new one's, via realtime.send in broadcast_changes' payload shape. This is the one generic split; the moves area drops its own files-only split.
  - *Evidence:* 0098 fn_realtime_broadcast: v_row := COALESCE(to_jsonb(NEW), to_jsonb(OLD)), with a single topic and broadcast_changes(…, NEW, OLD). The moves area's checked file move (answer 1: a private project's file moved into a shared project) is exactly this shape, and the moves and bodies areas both asked for a split.
- **SEC-5 [major] — taken.** R3 adds scans S1 to S6. S1: exactly one permissive SELECT policy on projects, carrying the privacy arm, and no restrictive or FOR ALL policy. S2: every permissive policy on every client-privileged project-scoped table hops through its anchor column (regex), or calls a gate on the allow-list. S3: two-anchor tables check both anchors. S4: a table with an unknown FK into a project-scoped table fails. S5: same-project references are covered. S6: the Moth personal arm. Each scan has a planted control, for example a broad second SELECT policy on projects.
  - *Evidence:* 0072 projects_select is the only SELECT policy on projects (0004 dropped 0002's). Permissive policies OR together, so a second SELECT policy would widen every hop. 0098 post-condition 11a and its regex (`FROM projects p\s+WHERE \(p\.id = <t>\.project_id\)`) are the precedent.
- **SEC-6 [major] — taken.** R5: on UPDATE, for bound callers, the guard refuses a change of producer_id to a non-NULL value unless can_manage_project_roster(OLD.id) (42501, plain words). Clearing the producer stays allowed. Client (R11): the Producer select in ProjectDetailPanel is disabled unless canOnProject(ctx,'project.roster.manage'). Suite 24's seating probes stay green for admins and managers.
  - *Evidence:* 0020 fn_projects_auto_staff (DEFINER, AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OF producer_id) seats NEW.producer_id as 'manager'. 0013 projects_update USING is can_write_project(id), which admits member seats and anyone on an unstaffed project. 0037's money gate opens on a manager seat. ProjectDetailPanel.jsx's Producer select is shown to anyone who can open the panel. 'project.roster.manage' exists in projectRoleMatrix.js.
- **SEC-7 [major] — taken.** R5 and R10. New column projects.topic_epoch int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0. The guard bumps it, for every caller, when visibility narrows. The topic becomes 'rabbit:project:<id>' at epoch 0 and 'rabbit:project:<id>:<n>' after a bump, so unchanged projects and old clients keep working. Both realtime.messages policies are restated to require project_topic_matches(topic). Both senders use project_topic(). On a flip, a payload-free AUDIENCE goes to the old topic. The client joins the epoch topic and re-joins when the index shows a new epoch. Narrowing by role or membership change stays a recorded, pre-existing residual.
  - *Evidence:* 0016 rabbit_project_topic_read and presence_write authorise at join through can_read_project_topic(split_part(topic,':',3)). RabbitProvider keeps channels across token refreshes (wsAuthKey comment). Builds older than this change bind neither AUDIENCE nor the new effect, so they keep a joined channel open after a flip. Only 0098 fn_realtime_broadcast and 0101 fn_budget_versions_broadcast send to the project topic. 'Realtime caches the join' is INFERRED, but the fix does not depend on it.
- **SEC-8 [minor] — taken.** R9: the side flags are computed first. The event shape is chosen (both sides, NEW only as INSERT, OLD only as DELETE, or nothing). The tasks filter then tests only the sides being sent, and the assets churn filter applies only to the both-sides UPDATE shape. A body probe checks that the side flags come before the filters.
  - *Evidence:* 0018 fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast: the tasks filter reads the assignee and reviewer on NEW and OLD, and the assets filter compares NEW and OLD, both before any side judgement.
- **SEC-9 [minor] — partly.** Composite FKs are rejected: they need unique keys on every parent, validation of unmeasured existing data, and NO ACTION cascades on moves. Instead, R6 adds one DEFINER guard with a fixed map. For client roles it checks only new or changed references (for arrays, only added elements, so existing orphans pass), and it skips under wilson.checked_move. files.folder_id and folders.parent_id become the moves area's DEFERRABLE composite FKs; the guard covers them until then. Scan S5 pins the coverage.
  - *Evidence:* Single-column FKs with no same-project check: 0034 tasks.phase_id, 0097 tasks.asset_id, 0000 assets.phase_id, 0063 phases.parent_phase_id, 0067 milestones.phase_id, 0040 shots.scene_id, 0041 folders(parent_id and the entity links), 0043 and 0000 files links. 0099 says the uuid[] links on assets (scene_ids, shot_ids, level_ids, experience_ids) have no FK and keep orphans. Only task_items (0096) and 0084/0091 use composite keys.
- **SEC-10 [minor] — partly.** Not fixed in Session 1. Coarsening it changes the members' upload refusal, which is not a privacy-repair decision. R15 now names it as a residual that personal spaces widen (their bodies count in company storage), and a line is added to S4b-06 for the owner's ruling. D1's 'only two items' is withdrawn.
  - *Evidence:* 0055 workspace_storage_usage() is DEFINER and executable by authenticated. Its caller uploadNotices.js (members' 'storage full' courtesy check) needs the exact figure. docs/OUTSTANDING.md S4b-06 already records it as a product decision, owned by the next storage session.
- **PR-01 [blocker] — taken.** S2 matches `(FROM|JOIN) \(?projects [a-z_]+` on the deparsed clause, together with the anchor column. The exception list is whatever the rolled-back rehearsal's scan produces, pinned with a reason for each entry.
  - *Evidence:* 0061 phase_deps_* hop through 'public.phases pre JOIN public.projects pp', and 0013 ingestion_chunks_* through 'public.ingestion_runs r JOIN public.projects p'. Neither prints the literal 'FROM projects'.
- **PR-02 [blocker] — taken.** S2 exempts any table where authenticated holds no SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE or DELETE (has_table_privilege), which also covers moth_history and file_moves later. A's post-condition skips edit_history, and B's post-condition and suite 111 check it. The allow-listed gates are named explicitly per table.
  - *Evidence:* 0093 gateway_ticket_mints has project_id, ENABLE and FORCE RLS, REVOKE ALL from PUBLIC, anon and authenticated, and no policy. edit_history_select gains its arm only in B.
- **PR-03 [major] — taken.** 0107's commit retargets suite 83 probe 20 to lives_ok, with a new control: a1 is untouched and b1 changed. It also updates 83's header and the OUTSTANDING C-R2-01 text. The rehearsal runs the whole set.
  - *Evidence:* Suite 83 probe 20 throws_ok on `UPDATE public.files SET description = 'x'` (42501), followed by a presence control counting a1 and b1 with NULL description = 2. With a USING hop, a1 is never admitted, so the statement succeeds and rewrites b1. A grep of every suite for column-free writes on the restated tables found only 83:319 and 90:398 (90's lives_ok stays true).
- **PR-04 [major] — taken.** Merged with SEC-2: P1's guard restatement refuses with 42501 and plain words before the CHECK is reached. Suite 110 (extended by P1) adds owner and admin flip probes with a planted control.
  - *Evidence:* The draft's D4 guard exempted admins and the owner. supabaseAdapter.js PROJECT_COLUMNS includes 'is_private', and RabbitProvider re-sends whole rows. The personal area's CHECK makes the flip fail with 23514.
- **PR-05 [major] — taken.** R12 is rewritten. is_private gives personal spaces owner-plus-admin RLS visibility, which is the floor answer 4 needs. Owner-only in Moth is moth_can_read_target's job (personal area). Exclusion from admins' ordinary lists is the personal area's listProjects filter, recorded as a dependency, not as 'covered'. Scan S6 and the R13 rule cover Moth tables.
  - *Evidence:* 0072 projects_select, 0082 passes_project_privacy and 0016 can_read_project_topic all carry the admin arm. The personal area keeps personal spaces out of ordinary lists only through a client filter at listProjects.
- **PR-06 [minor] — taken.** Suite 110 uses a probe shape per table: comments move by entity_id, project_members use a seat whose user has none on Q, folders use a non-root fixture, and the composite-FK tables use fixtures with no dependents. R2's rationale now says that composite FKs, and pg-safeupdate where it is preloaded, already refuse many move-out statements; the hop is defence in depth.
  - *Evidence:* 0000 comments has no project_id. 0013 project_members has PK (project_id, user_id). 0041 has folders_project_path_uniq. 0096, 0091 and 0084 composite FKs are ON UPDATE NO ACTION. 0096 fn_tasks_seed_items gives tasks items.
- **PR-07 [minor] — taken.** R8's rationale is corrected: cascaded child deletes stamp NULL (admins only). Both narrowings, cascaded children and trashed-project history becoming admin-only, are listed as accepted, fail-closed behaviour changes. Suite 111 probes both.
  - *Evidence:* 0061 capture is an AFTER trigger and resolves parents live, so for a cascade-deleted child the parent is already gone. 0016 can_read_project_topic is false for a trashed project (projects_select requires deleted_at IS NULL).
- **PR-08 [minor] — taken.** The re-run note names 0092's post-conditions 10 and 12 (and 0098's md5 pre-flight after C). 0108 restates passes_project_privacy's COMMENT to add can_access_project_money and edit_history_project_of. The OUTSTANDING fix-shape lines that point at '0107 / suite 110' (avatars, UNC CHECK, bin_files audio) move to the next free numbers.
  - *Evidence:* 0092 post-condition 10 pins edit_history_select whole and strpos-checks the snapshot body, and post-condition 12 pins 0037's money body. The 0092 COMMENT on passes_project_privacy lists its callers.
- **PR-09 [minor] — taken.** The premise is relabelled: the client sends created_by on every save, and the guard's silent pin makes it inert. PROJECT_COLUMNS is left alone (no churn). topic_epoch is deliberately not added to PROJECT_COLUMNS, and the guard pins it too.
  - *Evidence:* supabaseAdapter.js PROJECT_COLUMNS ends with 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at'…, and updateProject passes whole rows through toColumns.
- **PR-10 [minor] — partly.** The loss is accepted and recorded in R15 and handbook §4.5. The creator's and admins' other windows refresh their own private projects on join refetch, on AUDIENCE, and on the project topic while open. Refuted: a payload-free 'INDEX' ping on private-project writes would tell every member that hidden projects exist and when they are worked on, which her ruling forbids. AUDIENCE does not, because it fires only when one side is visible to everyone.
  - *Evidence:* 0018 header ('projects — index liveness'). handleWorkspaceEvent removes the project on deleted_at. The draft's D7 sends nothing for a private-both-sides change.
- **PR-11 [minor] — taken.** R11: a revalidateRef flag read and cleared by the debounced run, refreshProjectsIndex returning the list behind a sequence number, and the close also calling saveRabbitSettings({activeProjectId:null}). R14 orders B after A because B's pre-flight requires 0107.
  - *Evidence:* RabbitProvider.jsx: refreshProjectsIndex returns nothing and has no sequence guard. scheduleProjectsIndexRefetch clears one shared timer (wsIndexRefetchTimerRef). The project-trashed branch never calls saveRabbitSettings. B's real dependency on A is its pre-flight, not the runner's fixtures (the runner bypasses RLS).

#### Decisions

- **R1** Session 1 fixes the whole measured class before any Moth table: write hops on every clause (including the two leaking reads), the projects guard, same-project references, the money gate, edit history, the workspace channel, the per-project split and the topic epoch. Residuals in OUTSTANDING (R15): join-cache narrowing by role or membership change (pre-existing, bounded by token life, INFERRED); S4b-06 storage totals (the owner's call); index liveness lost for private projects (accepted).
  - *Why:* Plan rule: security defects are fixed. Moth's catalogue, glow, counts, trash and history all assume project visibility is a boundary, and her ruling is that nothing appears that a person cannot already see.
- **R2** Every client write clause (INSERT WITH CHECK, UPDATE USING and WITH CHECK, DELETE USING) of tasks, assets, comments, folders, scenes, shots, levels, experiences, project_members, files (update and delete), milestones (update USING, delete) and task_templates carries the hop `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = <t>.project_id)`, evaluated under the caller's projects_select. Variants: comments use p.id = public.fn_comment_project_id(comments.entity_type, comments.entity_id), where the kind is proven by the id being found in the table the kind names; task_templates use `project_id IS NULL OR EXISTS(...)`. project_members_select (0020) and task_templates_select (0044) gain the same hop. task_deps_update and phase_deps_update gain a WITH CHECK equal to their INSERT check (both endpoints, under RLS).
  - *Why:* WITH CHECK alone does not stop a filter-less, column-free UPDATE … SET project_id = <public> or DELETE: only USING judges them. Composite FKs (task_items, bins, shot lists) and pg-safeupdate already refuse many of these statements, so the hop is defence in depth that does not depend on either. Read-side: rosters and pinned templates of a private project reach every member today.
- **R3** Catalogue scans. Each runs in 0107's post-condition over what exists at A, edit_history excepted, and as suite 110 probes at the tip, where it covers every later table. S1: public.projects has exactly one permissive SELECT policy, projects_select, whose qual contains is_private, created_by = auth.uid(), current_app_role() = 'admin' and deleted_at IS NULL, and no restrictive or FOR ALL policy. S2: for every public base table with a project_id column, or listed in the anchor map {asset_versions: asset_id→assets, task_links: task_id→tasks, task_dependencies: predecessor_id+successor_id→tasks, phase_dependencies: predecessor_id+successor_id→phases, ingestion_chunks: run_id→ingestion_runs, comments: fn_comment_project_id}, where authenticated holds any of SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE or DELETE: every permissive policy's every non-null clause either matches `(FROM|JOIN) \(?projects [a-z_]+` together with its anchor column, or hops through an anchor parent, or calls an allow-listed gate (budget_versions, budget_lines, budget_actuals, expenses and project_rate_overrides: can_access_project_money(project_id); file_events and edit_history (from B): can_read_project_topic(project_id); upload_reservations: own-row arm). The list the rehearsal produces is pinned with a reason per entry. S3: the effective check (with_check, or qual when with_check is NULL) of every INSERT and UPDATE policy on a two-anchor table names both anchors. S4: a client-writable table without project_id that has a single-column FK into a project-scoped table and is in neither the anchor map nor a pinned exception list (for example rate_cards.source_file_id, workspace-level) fails. S5: every single-column FK between two project_id tables (other than project_id→projects) is either composite with project_id or in R6's map, with its trigger attached. S6 (suite only): every client-privileged table named moth_% has, on every permissive policy, a call to moth_can_read_target or moth_can_write_target, or a personal_owner_id owner comparison in an arm without current_app_role.
  - *Why:* The S22 lesson: scan the catalogue, not a list. These scans catch a second SELECT policy, a one-endpoint hop, an unhopped new table and a bare D2 policy on a Moth table. They exempt definer-only tables (gateway_ticket_mints, moth_history, file_moves).
- **R4** projects_update is 0013's body, with USING gaining `AND (NOT is_private OR created_by = auth.uid() OR public.current_app_role() = 'admin')`. WITH CHECK is unchanged.
  - *Why:* can_write_project admits every app manager to every project, and a filter-less UPDATE reads no column, so projects_select never judges it.
- **R5** One guard, fn_projects_owner_guard() with trigger trg_projects_owner_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON projects: plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER, SET search_path = public, EXECUTE revoked from PUBLIC, anon and authenticated. The bound caller test is COALESCE(current_setting('role', true),'') IN ('authenticated','anon'). The role setting is unchanged inside client-called definer RPCs (soft_delete_row, ensure_personal_space), so they are bound; the runner and service_role are not. Bound INSERT: NEW.created_by := auth.uid(); NEW.topic_epoch := 0. Bound UPDATE: NEW.created_by := OLD.created_by and NEW.topic_epoch := OLD.topic_epoch (silent pins). A change of is_private is refused (42501) unless OLD.created_by = auth.uid() or current_app_role() = 'admin'. A change of producer_id to a non-NULL value is refused (42501) unless COALESCE(can_manage_project_roster(OLD.id), false). For every caller, a narrowing (OLD.is_private IS NOT TRUE AND NEW.is_private, or NEW.is_private AND created_by changed) sets NEW.topic_epoch := OLD.topic_epoch + 1. Messages: 'Only the person who made this project, or an admin, can change who can see it.' and 'Only someone who manages this project's team can choose its producer.' The personal area restates this function rather than adding fn_projects_identity_guard.
  - *Why:* MEASURED: fn_audit_touch (0001) stamps created_by only when it is NULL, and no projects trigger guards it. Private visibility hangs on created_by. A member seat can mint a manager seat through producer_id (0020). The role-setting test binds inside client-called definers too, which the personal area's no-binning rule needs, while leaving runner fixtures alone. A second projects guard would duplicate the created_by pin and the flip rule.
- **R6** New fn_same_project_refs_guard(), DEFINER, search_path pinned, revoked from clients, attached as trg_<t>_same_project_refs BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE on tasks, assets, phases, milestones, shots, folders, files, task_dependencies and phase_dependencies. It returns NEW unless the caller is bound (as in R5) and current_setting('wilson.checked_move', true) is not 'on'. Fixed map: tasks{asset_id:assets, phase_id:phases}; assets{phase_id:phases, scene_ids:scenes[], shot_ids:shots[], level_ids:levels[], experience_ids:experiences[]}; phases{parent_phase_id:phases}; milestones{phase_id:phases}; shots{scene_id:scenes}; folders{parent_id:folders, asset_id, scene_id, shot_id, level_id, experience_id}; files{folder_id, phase_id, asset_id, task_id, scene_id, shot_id, level_id, experience_id}. On INSERT, or when the column or project_id changed, each non-NULL value (for arrays, each element added since OLD) must exist with project_id = NEW.project_id, via `EXECUTE format('SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.%I WHERE id = $1 AND project_id = $2)', <map table>)`. Otherwise it raises 23503: 'That item belongs to another project or no longer exists.' Dependency rows: both endpoints exist in one project, else the same error. Missing and other-project cases answer identically, so the guard is no oracle. The rehearsal derives the final map from pg_constraint, and S5 pins it.
  - *Why:* Secondary links and arrays let a Q writer who knows a P id attach Q rows to private structure, or carry P ids into Q readers and the workspace payload. Checking only new or changed values avoids validating unmeasured existing data, and existing orphans in arrays pass.
- **R7** can_access_project_money (CREATE OR REPLACE from 0037, same signature and grants) gains `AND public.passes_project_privacy(p_project)` between the workspace EXISTS and the role arm. Its COMMENT and passes_project_privacy's COMMENT are restated (callers now include can_access_project_money and edit_history_project_of).
  - *Why:* One change reaches all 8 money storage policies, the 20 budget-table clauses, the files and file_events money arms, edit_history_select, can_access_project_legal's money half, fn_projects_budget_settings_guard, fn_projects_open_budget_version_guard and reserve_upload_bytes. A non-creator manager seat on a private project loses money access, which her ruling (private = creator and admins) already settles.
- **R8** Edit history. (a) New edit_history_project_of(p_entity_type text, p_row jsonb) RETURNS uuid: plpgsql STABLE DEFINER, revoked from clients, any error returns NULL. Mapping: projects→id; phases, assets, tasks, files, ingestion_runs→project_id; comments→fn_comment_project_id; asset_versions→assets via asset_id; task_links→tasks via task_id; ingestion_chunks→ingestion_runs via run_id; task_dependencies and phase_dependencies→the shared project of both endpoints, else NULL; rate_cards and rate_card_entries→NULL. (b) fn_edit_history_capture is restated from 0061 word for word, plus: v_pn := resolver(NEW), v_po := resolver(OLD); v_project := INSERT→v_pn, DELETE→v_po, UPDATE→CASE WHEN v_pn IS NOT DISTINCT FROM v_po THEN v_pn END. v_priv := for projects, (OLD.is_private OR NEW.is_private); otherwise COALESCE((SELECT is_private FROM projects WHERE id = v_project), true); and false for rate cards. project_id and captured_private are added to the INSERT. 'topic_epoch' joins c_noise. (c) New column edit_history.captured_private boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false. (d) fn_edit_history_money_snapshot is restated from 0092 with the move line (SEC-1). (e) The backfill goes through edit_history_project_at(p_entity_type, p_entity_id, p_row jsonb, p_at timestamptz), a revoked DEFINER function kept for replay. An update row whose own diff carries its anchor key gets NULL. Otherwise the project at p_at is the 'old' of the first later row of the same entity (or, for child types, of its parent) whose diff carries project_id, falling back to the live row, then to the entity's delete row; dependencies require both endpoints equal. captured_private at p_at is the 'old' of the first later projects row whose diff carries is_private, else the current value, else true. It fills NULL project_id rows (rate cards excepted), re-derives files rows only for entities with a recorded project change, and sets captured_private on every row with a project. (f) edit_history_select is 0092's body plus `AND (entity_type IN ('rate_cards','rate_card_entries') OR public.current_app_role() = 'admin' OR (public.can_read_project_topic(project_id) AND (NOT captured_private OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = edit_history.project_id AND p.created_by = auth.uid()))))`.
  - *Why:* A history row must be readable only by people who could see both sides when it happened. A single project_id column cannot hold two audiences, so a cross-project row goes to admins only. Retention purges oldest-first, so the 'first later change' walk is exact inside the window and needs no create row. Accepted narrowings, all fail-closed: cascaded child deletes and trashed projects' history become admin-only. Suite 95 probe 171 keeps one trigger on edit_history.
- **R9** fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast is restated from 0018, keeping the probe, the workspace_id read and the EXCEPTION guard. Steps: v_new := to_jsonb(NEW); v_old := to_jsonb(OLD). Side flags: workspace_members→the side is present; projects→the side is present AND NOT (side->>'is_private')::boolean; tasks, assets and project_members→the side is present AND EXISTS (projects p WHERE p.id = side project_id AND NOT p.is_private). A projects UPDATE whose flags differ sends realtime.send(jsonb_build_object('table','projects','schema','public','operation','AUDIENCE','record',NULL,'old_record',NULL), 'AUDIENCE', 'rabbit:workspace:'||v_ws, true) and returns. Shape: both sides→the 0018 call (TG_OP, NEW, OLD); new only→INSERT-shaped; old only→DELETE-shaped; neither→return. Downgraded shapes go through realtime.send in broadcast_changes' payload shape, with only the visible side's jsonb. Filters are then applied: tasks skip when no SENT side has an assignee or reviewer; the assets churn filter applies only to the both-sides UPDATE. COMMENTs are restated on this function and on can_read_workspace_topic ('membership ≡ visibility' has been false since 0072).
  - *Why:* broadcast_changes has no per-recipient filter, and the topic's join gate is membership only. AUDIENCE fires only when one side is visible to every member, so it reveals nothing new. Clients learn the truth through RLS. Personal spaces are is_private by the personal area's CHECK and never flip, so they never reach this topic.
- **R10** Per-project topic. New project_topic(p_project uuid) RETURNS text: STABLE DEFINER, revoked from clients; it returns 'rabbit:project:'||id||CASE WHEN topic_epoch > 0 THEN ':'||topic_epoch ELSE '' END, or NULL when the project is gone. New project_topic_matches(p_topic text) RETURNS boolean: SECURITY INVOKER sql, GRANT authenticated. It is true when a project visible under the caller's RLS has id = fn_try_uuid(split_part(p_topic,':',3)), and split_part 4 equals '' at epoch 0 or the epoch text otherwise, and split_part 5 is ''. Both 0016 realtime.messages policies are restated, inside a guarded DO, with `AND public.project_topic_matches(realtime.topic())`. fn_realtime_broadcast is restated from 0098 (with an md5 pre-flight accepting 0098's or this file's body): every arm is kept; each side's project is resolved, dependencies only when both endpoints match, the files money test first; same project→broadcast_changes(project_topic(p), TG_OP, …, NEW, OLD) as before; different projects→DELETE-shaped (NULL, OLD) to the old topic and INSERT-shaped (NEW, NULL) to the new one, via realtime.send. Projects arm: the topic comes from the row's own topic_epoch; when OLD.topic_epoch differs from NEW, a payload-free AUDIENCE goes to the old topic and the UPDATE to the new one. fn_budget_versions_broadcast is restated from 0101 with the topic := public.project_topic(v_project), keeping the thin {id, project_id} record and not calling broadcast_changes.
  - *Why:* A move out of a private project must not carry private values to the public topic. A subscriber who joined before a narrowing must stop hearing the project, whatever their build. Epoch 0 keeps today's topic, so nothing changes for projects that never narrowed.
- **R11** Client changes. Adapter: subscribeProjectChanges(projectId, cb, opts) uses opts.topicEpoch (default 0) for the channel name and binds 'AUDIENCE' beside INSERT, UPDATE and DELETE, forwarding {table:'projects', op:'AUDIENCE', record:null, oldRecord:null}; subscribeWorkspaceChanges binds 'AUDIENCE' the same way; listProjects selects topic_epoch behind a column probe in the privateProjectsAvailable shape. realtimeMerge.applyEventCore: op 'AUDIENCE', or a projects UPDATE with record.is_private true and oldRecord?.is_private !== true, adds {type:'audience'}. RabbitProvider: requestIndexRevalidation() sets revalidateRef.current = true and schedules the debounced refetch. The debounced run reads and clears the flag. refreshProjectsIndex returns the list behind a sequence number (a stale result is dropped). If the flag was set and the open project is missing, it runs the project-trashed teardown, calls saveRabbitSettings({activeProjectId:null}) and shows 'This project is no longer shared with you.' through the existing notice surface. handleWorkspaceEvent (projects AUDIENCE) and the 'audience' effect call it. A workspace projects row with is_private === true is never merged; it triggers a revalidation instead. The project-channel effect depends on projectsIndex[activeProjectId]?.topic_epoch, so it re-joins on a new epoch. ProjectDetailPanel: the Producer select is disabled unless canOnProject(access,'project.roster.manage'). useMyTasks needs no change (MEASURED: evt.table === 'projects' already reloads).
  - *Why:* RLS is the boundary, and the client cannot know entitlement locally. The flag-in-a-ref and the sequence guard close PR-11's coalescing loss.
- **R12** Personal spaces are covered here only through is_private (personal area's projects_personal_shape_chk): projects_select, passes_project_privacy, every hop, the money gate, both broadcasts and the epoch. This gives owner-plus-admin RLS visibility, which answer 4 needs. The owner-only rules belong to the personal area: Moth through moth_can_read_target, the listProjects exclusion, P1's guard and projects_update restatements, and the seat guard.
  - *Why:* One privacy flag keeps suppression in one place. The draft's claim that the admin arm 'covers' personal spaces contradicted 'admins from Files only'.
- **R13** Deferred spec for Moth tables (Sessions 2 to 4): every project-scoped Moth policy carries the R2 hop on every clause, and for rows that can live in a personal space also an owner-only arm with no admin leg (through moth_can_read_target or moth_can_write_target, or `personal_owner_id = auth.uid()`). Personal-space Moth rows never ride the project topic, and project-scoped Moth broadcasts use project_topic(). Moth caches keyed by project (graph, glow, trash) subscribe to the provider's revalidation and drop nodes whose project leaves the index. Session 1 creates no such table (answer 6).
  - *Why:* An admin can join a personal space's project topic and read it through RLS, so Moth's owner-only promise must live in Moth's own arms. Scan S6 fails a table that forgets.
- **R14** Three migrations, each one transaction with no enum, each with its own suite in the 82/83 shape (no new table, so no RLS_TABLES line): A 0107_private_project_write_hops (suite 110), B 0108_private_project_money_and_history (suite 111), C 0109_private_project_channels (suite 112, with the client changes and vitest). Each is registered in rls.yml's replay list and rlsSuiteLists.test.js's tail pins (listed.slice(-5), picked.slice(-6)) in its own commit. All three land before P1 (personal), the moves, bodies and NAS migrations and moth_history. Numbers are remeasured at the tip.
  - *Why:* The personal area's P0 prerequisite: without these, the first ensure_personal_space INSERT would be broadcast to every member. Separate files keep each rehearsal and its planted controls small.
- **R15** OUTSTANDING edits. Remove A-R1-04, A-R2-03, C-R2-01's remaining bullet, S4d-01 and S4d-02. Add: (1) join-cache narrowing by app-role demotion or membership deactivation (INFERRED, pre-existing, bounded by token life; settled by a wilson-dev measurement); (2) S4b-06 gains a line saying personal-space bodies now count in the total, waiting on the owner; (3) index liveness of private projects in creators' and admins' other windows rests on join refetch, AUDIENCE and the project topic (accepted); (4) re-run order: re-run 0107 after any replay of 0013, 0020, 0040, 0041, 0044, 0061 or 0082; 0108 after 0037, 0061 or 0092; 0109 after 0016, 0018, 0098 or 0101. 0092 refuses after 0108 (post-conditions 10 and 12), and 0098 refuses after 0109 (md5). (5) The definer re-measure result: every SECURITY DEFINER body calling a write gate also calls passes_project_privacy or reads under a hop.
  - *Why:* Residuals are named, with the measurement that settles each one.

#### Schema changes

- **public.projects.topic_epoch (A)** (restates from: new): ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS topic_epoch integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, with CHECK (topic_epoch >= 0) as DROP IF EXISTS + ADD. COMMENT: written only by trg_projects_owner_guard; read by project_topic and project_topic_matches.
- **public.fn_projects_owner_guard() + trg_projects_owner_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.projects (A)** (restates from: new): Body as in R5. DEFINER, search_path = public, REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated. The COMMENTs on projects.is_private and created_by are restated (who may change them). Post-conditions on the comment-stripped body: 'NEW.created_by := OLD.created_by', 'NEW.topic_epoch := OLD.topic_epoch + 1', 'can_manage_project_roster(OLD.id)', "current_setting('role', true)"; the trigger is enabled, BEFORE, ROW, INSERT and UPDATE; no client EXECUTE.
- **policy projects_update (A)** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql): 0013 body; USING gains the R4 arm; WITH CHECK unchanged. Post-condition: the whole qual compared as deparsed.
- **policies tasks_insert/update/delete, assets_*, comments_* (A)** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql): 0013 bodies word for word, plus the R2 hop on every clause (comments via fn_comment_project_id). tasks_select (0097) and assets_select untouched.
- **policies folders_* (A)** (restates from: 0041_folders.sql): 0041 bodies plus the hop on every clause.
- **policies scenes_*, shots_*, levels_*, experiences_* insert/update/delete (A)** (restates from: 0040_scenes_shots_levels_experiences.sql): 0040 bodies plus the hop on every clause.
- **policies project_members_select / _insert / _update / _delete (A)** (restates from: 0020_admin_grants_and_alignment.sql (select); 0013_project_members.sql (writes)): select: the 0020 body plus the hop. Writes: the 0013 bodies plus the hop on every clause. No recursion: projects_select calls only definer helpers.
- **policies files_update / files_delete (A)** (restates from: 0092_legal_gate_managers.sql): 0092 bodies word for word (money and Legal arms). files_update USING gains the hop (its WITH CHECK already has it, from 0083); files_delete USING gains the hop.
- **policies milestones_update / milestones_delete (A)** (restates from: 0082_private_project_definers.sql (update); 0067_milestones.sql (delete)): update: the 0082 §3b body with the hop added to USING. delete: the 0067 body plus the hop.
- **policies task_templates_select/insert/update/delete (A)** (restates from: 0044_task_templates.sql): 0044 bodies plus `AND (project_id IS NULL OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = task_templates.project_id))` on every clause.
- **policies task_deps_update (A), phase_deps_update (A)** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql (task_deps_update, insert check text); 0061_phase_dependencies.sql (phase_deps_update, insert check text)): USING unchanged; each gains a WITH CHECK equal to its own table's INSERT WITH CHECK (predecessor writable under RLS, successor visible under RLS).
- **public.fn_same_project_refs_guard() + trg_<t>_same_project_refs on tasks, assets, phases, milestones, shots, folders, files, task_dependencies, phase_dependencies (A)** (restates from: new): As in R6. DEFINER, search_path pinned, revoked from clients. BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE FOR EACH ROW. Its name sorts after the existing Legal and body guards, so their refusals keep their words.
- **Migration A pre-flight and post-conditions** (restates from: new): Pre-flight RAISEs unless 0072 (projects.is_private), 0082 (passes_project_privacy) and 0092 (files_delete names can_access_project_legal) are present. Post-conditions: each restated clause keeps every prior gate (current_workspace_id, has_active_membership where it had it, its write gate, files' money and Legal arms) and names the hop (by regex); each touched table keeps exactly its prior permissive policy names; scans S1 to S5 over the current catalogue, edit_history excepted; both triggers present.
- **public.can_access_project_money(uuid) (B)** (restates from: 0037_budget_system.sql (body); 0092_legal_gate_managers.sql (passes_project_privacy COMMENT)): R7 body: LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public, grants unchanged. COMMENTs restated on it and on passes_project_privacy. Post-conditions: the whole body is pinned with whitespace collapsed, passes_project_privacy appears once, and can_access_project_legal is still 0092's body.
- **public.edit_history.captured_private (B)** (restates from: 0088_legal_files.sql (project_id comment)): ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS captured_private boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false. COMMENT: whether the row's project was private when the change was captured; written by the capture and the 0108 backfill. The COMMENT on edit_history.project_id is restated (0088 said 'files rows only').
- **public.edit_history_project_of(text, jsonb), public.edit_history_project_at(text, uuid, jsonb, timestamptz) (B)** (restates from: new): As in R8 (a) and (e). plpgsql STABLE DEFINER, search_path pinned, REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, GRANT service_role. Both refuse an entity_type outside 0061's 14-name allowlist (they return NULL), and any error returns NULL.
- **public.fn_edit_history_capture() (B)** (restates from: 0061_phase_dependencies.sql): 0061 body word for word, plus R8 (b). EXCEPTION→WARNING kept; REVOKE re-applied; the 14 trg_<t>_edit_history triggers unchanged. Post-conditions on the comment-stripped body: all 0061 parent-join tables, the resolver called for both sides, project_id and captured_private in the INSERT, 'topic_epoch' in c_noise, the EXCEPTION guard.
- **public.fn_edit_history_money_snapshot() (B)** (restates from: 0092_legal_gate_managers.sql): 0092 body word for word (its post-condition-10 strings intact) plus `IF NEW.action = 'update' AND NEW.diff ? 'project_id' THEN NEW.project_id := NULL; END IF;` after the classifier SELECT. Trigger trg_edit_history_money unchanged.
- **edit_history backfill (B)** (restates from: new): UPDATE through edit_history_project_at, as in R8 (e). Fills NULL project_id; re-derives files rows only for entities with a recorded project change; sets captured_private on every row with a project. The rehearsal records row counts before and after.
- **policy edit_history_select (B)** (restates from: 0092_legal_gate_managers.sql): 0092 body word for word plus the R8 (f) arm; COMMENT restated. Post-condition: the whole qual is pinned.
- **public.project_topic(uuid), public.project_topic_matches(text) (C)** (restates from: new): As in R10. project_topic: STABLE DEFINER, revoked from clients. project_topic_matches: INVOKER sql STABLE, REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon; GRANT authenticated.
- **realtime.messages policies rabbit_project_topic_read, rabbit_project_topic_presence_write (C)** (restates from: 0016_realtime_broadcast.sql): Restated from 0016 inside a guarded DO (NOTICE when realtime.messages is absent), with `AND public.project_topic_matches(realtime.topic())` added to each.
- **public.fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast() (C)** (restates from: 0018_workspace_channel.sql): As in R9; triggers unchanged; COMMENTs on it and on can_read_workspace_topic restated.
- **public.fn_realtime_broadcast() (C)** (restates from: 0098_table_views.sql): As in R10; md5 pre-flight (0098's e5abdcf2b8abce914416acb148144971 or this file's own); every table arm and trigger kept.
- **public.fn_budget_versions_broadcast() (C)** (restates from: 0101_versions_and_items_realtime.sql): 0101 body with only the topic expression changed to public.project_topic(v_project) (NULL→return).
- **Migration C post-conditions** (restates from: new): Comment-stripped bodies: the workspace broadcast names is_private, 'AUDIENCE' and 'rabbit:workspace:', computes its side flags before the assignee and reviewer filters (position order), and builds its AUDIENCE payload from literals only. fn_realtime_broadcast names project_topic( and realtime.send, compares both sides' projects, and keeps every 0098 table name and the file_row_is_money test on NEW and OLD. fn_budget_versions_broadcast has no broadcast_changes and no to_jsonb(NEW). Both realtime policies (when realtime.messages is present) name project_topic_matches. The five ws triggers exist. No client EXECUTE on any definer. budget_versions has no whole-row trigger. fn_task_items_ws_broadcast still names is_private.

- **WILSON/supabase/tests/rls/100_no_main_item.sql probe 7; 101_table_views.sql probe 18 (0109 commit)**: Add 0109's md5 of fn_realtime_broadcast to the accepted set (101's probe names 0109's body). Every later restatement (moves, NAS) does the same.
