Pair checks: (1) an admin makes a shared project private while a teammate has it open, on an old build and a new one: the teammate's list and open project close with the exact notice, the creator's do not, and the old build stops hearing the project; (2) a member seat cannot choose a producer.

#### Client changes

- **WILSON/src/tools/rabbit_v0.1.0/adapters/supabaseAdapter.js subscribeProjectChanges, subscribeWorkspaceChanges, listProjects**: Channel name `rabbit:project:${id}${epoch > 0 ? ':' + epoch : ''}` from opts.topicEpoch. Both subscriptions bind 'AUDIENCE' and forward {table:'projects', op:'AUDIENCE', record:null, oldRecord:null}. listProjects adds ', topic_epoch' behind a cached column probe (the privateProjectsAvailable shape). topic_epoch is not added to PROJECT_COLUMNS. *(production caller: RabbitProvider's project-channel and workspace-channel effects and refreshProjectsIndex (existing callers; no new export).)*
- **WILSON/src/tools/rabbit_v0.1.0/state/realtimeMerge.js applyEventCore (projects branch) and the effect docstring**: op 'AUDIENCE', or a projects UPDATE flipping is_private to true, adds {type:'audience'}. *(production caller: RabbitProvider.handleRealtimeEvent → effects reducer.)*
- **WILSON/src/tools/rabbit_v0.1.0/state/RabbitProvider.jsx (refreshProjectsIndex, scheduleProjectsIndexRefetch, handleWorkspaceEvent, effects reducer, project-channel effect)**: As in R11: revalidateRef, a sequence-guarded refreshProjectsIndex returning the list, close plus saveRabbitSettings({activeProjectId:null}) plus the notice 'This project is no longer shared with you.', is_private rows never merged, re-join on a topic_epoch change, scheduleProjectsIndexRefetch reached through a ref so handleWorkspaceEvent's identity is stable. *(production caller: Existing channel callbacks; internal only.)*
- **WILSON/src/components/Projects/ProjectDetailPanel.jsx (Producer select) and ProjectsPage.jsx (prop)**: ProjectsPage computes canManageTeam = canOnProject(<the project's access ctx>, 'project.roster.manage') and passes it; the Producer Select is disabled with a plain hint when false. *(production caller: ProjectsPage → ProjectDetailPanel (existing).)*

#### Migration order

- **A: 0107_private_project_write_hops.sql + suite 110_private_project_write_hops.sql**: Pre-flight; projects.topic_epoch; fn_projects_owner_guard and its trigger; projects_update; R2 restatements; deps WITH CHECKs; fn_same_project_refs_guard and its triggers; post-conditions with scans S1 to S5. In the same commit: suite 110, suites 83, 90 and 95 (files pins), the replay line and the tail pins.
  - *Constraints:* Read drift first. Run the scan on wilson-dev in a rolled-back rehearsal to produce the exception and anchor lists and R6's map (from pg_constraint), then pin them. Count existing cross-project references there (recorded, not fixed). Rehearse tap-hosted.py with the migration and suite 110, collected = planned. Run the WHOLE pgTAP set (the hops refuse writes under trashed projects; the guards change persona fixtures): 03, 04, 06, 18, 19, 24, 48 to 52, 54, 68, 71, 80, 82, 83, 90, 92, 95, 99 to 102 must stay green. LF apply, history row with md5.
- **B: 0108_private_project_money_and_history.sql + suite 111_private_project_money_and_history.sql**: Pre-flight (0092's can_access_project_legal and edit_history.is_legal, and 0107, present); money gate; COMMENTs; captured_private; resolver and walk; capture; snapshot; backfill; edit_history_select; post-conditions. In the same commit: suite 111, suite 95 probes 17 and 167, replay and tail pins.
  - *Constraints:* Ordered after A by its pre-flight. The backfill is recorded before and after. The whole set must pass, including 17, 43 to 47, 53, 63, 77, 78, 90, 91, 92, 95 and 104. Record that 0092 is not re-runnable after B.
- **C: 0109_private_project_channels.sql + suite 112_private_project_channels.sql, with the client changes and vitest in the same commit**: project_topic, project_topic_matches, realtime.messages policies, both broadcasts, the budget-versions sender, COMMENTs, post-conditions; suites 100 and 101 md5 sets; client and vitest.
  - *Constraints:* Behaviour probes are vacuous on CI ('no-schema'); the rolled-back rehearsal on wilson-dev (realtime.messages present) is the bite, and its planted-control results go in the hand-off. Measure the join cache there (join, flip, write, observe) and record it. Bundle bytes before and after (expected under 20 KB). The client may ship before C (it handles a missing column and missing AUDIENCE).
- **Then other areas, in this order**: P1 personal (restates fn_projects_owner_guard and projects_update from 0107; adds the CHECK and the seat guard); NAS ALTER TYPE in its own migration; bodies, moves and NAS restate fn_realtime_broadcast from 0109 (each updating the 0109 md5 pre-flight chain and the suites 100 and 101 sets); moth_history.
  - *Constraints:* Each later project-scoped table is subject to suite 110's scans at the tip. Moth tables follow R13. Remeasure free numbers (the personal area's provisional suites shift past 112).

#### Tests

- **WILSON/supabase/tests/rls/110_private_project_write_hops.sql (new, replay list)**: Fixtures (runner): ws_a; creator C (app manager) of private P; second manager M2; admin A; member U; unstaffed private Pu by C; public Q; a row per table in P and Q, without dependents where composite FKs exist. Per table, with the PR-06 shapes (comments by entity_id; project_members a seat whose user has none on Q; folders non-root): M2's plain INSERT into P throws 42501 and into Q lives; C and A insert into P; M2's filter-less move into P throws; M2's filter-less move into Q lives while the runner sees the P row still in P; M2's filter-less DELETE lives while the P row remains and the Q row is gone; U's insert into Pu throws. M2 counts 0 of P's seats and templates; C sees them. projects: M2's filter-less SET title and SET is_private=false leave P unchanged. Guard: M2's SET created_by on Q leaves C; M2's INSERT naming created_by=C stores M2; M2 SET is_private=true on Q throws; C on Q and A on P live and bump topic_epoch by 1; a public→private flip bumps, private→public does not. A member seat's SET producer_id throws and the roster count is unchanged; an app manager's lives and seats the producer. Refs: a Q task with a P phase_id or asset_id, a Q asset adding a P level to level_ids, a Q→P dependency, a Q file with a P scene_id each throw 23503 with one message, the same as for a random uuid; a pre-existing orphan in level_ids passes on a whole-array resend. task_deps_update re-pointing successor_id to a P task throws. Scans S1 to S6. Planted controls, each restored by sha1: drop the hop from tasks_update USING, files_delete and comments_insert; remove the owner trigger; add a broad second SELECT policy on projects (S1 red); remove successor from task_deps_update's check (S3 red); create a temp moth_zz table with a bare hop policy (S6 red); drop one map entry (S5 red).
- **WILSON/supabase/tests/rls/111_private_project_money_and_history.sql (new, replay list)**: Money: M2 is seated manager on P and Q. As M2: can_access_project_money(P) false; P's budget_lines, expenses and budget_versions return 0 rows; storage.objects under projects/P/INVOICES and LEGAL return 0 rows in both buckets; can_access_project_legal(P) false; Q visible (control); C (seated) and A see P's money. History: one capture probe per entity type (14). A task moved P→Q: the move row has project_id NULL, and M2 reads no row naming P or carrying P-era values (planted: revert to NEW-only resolution, red). A file moved P→Q through the runner: the move row is NULL (snapshot line). A project flipped private then public: rows captured while private are readable by C and A, not M2 (captured_private). Walk: crafted rows (runner) before and after a recorded move resolve to the old and new project. Cascaded task_links delete: NULL, readable by A only. Trashed project: M2 0 rows, A all. rate_cards visible to M2. Resolver and walk closed to authenticated. Planted controls: remove passes_project_privacy from the money gate; remove the can_read_project_topic arm; remove the captured_private arm.
- **WILSON/supabase/tests/rls/112_private_project_channels.sql (new, replay list; suite 23's environment-tolerant shape)**: Count-delta helpers on realtime.messages answering skipped-ok, landed, no-schema or no-partition. Workspace topic: private P's insert, rename, assigned-task insert, asset trash and seat insert add nothing; the same on Q land; flip Q→private gives exactly one AUDIENCE with record NULL and neither Q's id nor its title in the payload; flip back gives one AUDIENCE; a P task moved to Q gives an INSERT shape with no P values; a Q→P move gives a DELETE shape with Q values only; SEC-8: a Q→P move gaining an assignee in P with none on Q sends nothing. Project topic: a P→Q asset move sends a DELETE shape to P's topic and an INSERT shape to Q's, and Q's payload holds no P id; after a flip of Q, nothing lands on 'rabbit:project:Q' and writes land on 'rabbit:project:Q:1', and the old topic gets one AUDIENCE; a cross-project dependency row sends nothing. CI-biting: project_topic_matches as M2 for 'rabbit:project:Q' true at epoch 0 and false at 1, ':1' false for M2 and true for C, a non-numeric suffix false; body-structure probes as in C's post-conditions; md5 pre-flight. Planted controls: drop the projects is_private test; replace AUDIENCE with broadcast_changes(NEW,OLD); drop the child EXISTS; drop the side comparison in fn_realtime_broadcast; drop project_topic_matches from the read policy.
- **WILSON/src/tools/rabbit_v0.1.0/state/realtimeMerge.test.js (extend)**: AUDIENCE and a projects is_private true←false UPDATE yield 'audience'; a public rename does not; a trashed project still yields project-trashed. Red on the pre-change source; planted: remove the arm.
- **Provider and adapter vitest: extend WILSON/src/tools/rabbit_v0.1.0/state/mountRabbit.test.jsx if its fake adapter carries both subscriptions, else a new state/projectAudience.test.jsx; adapter binding with a fake client.channel; WILSON/src/components/Projects/ProjectDetailPanel test (existing or new)**: An AUDIENCE event gives one listProjects call; a missing open project closes, clears the bundle, history and saved activeProjectId, and shows the notice; a listed one stays open (creator and admin case); a join refetch already pending plus AUDIENCE still revalidates (the flag survives coalescing); a stale refresh result is dropped. A workspace is_private row is not merged. An epoch change re-subscribes to ':1'. The adapter binds AUDIENCE on both channels and builds the epoch name. The Producer select is disabled without roster.manage. Each test is red first; plants remove each piece.

#### Risks

- The hops evaluate under projects_select, which hides trashed projects, so plain client writes to a trashed project's children are now refused.
  - *Mitigation:* Restore paths are definers (fn_trash_authz, 0082). Run the whole set (19, 71, 82). A racing write gets an ordinary 42501 that the optimistic rollback handles.
- The guard's role-setting test is assumed to read 'authenticated' inside client-called definers (soft_delete_row, ensure_personal_space).
  - *Mitigation:* Suite 110 probe: a definer path called as a persona keeps created_by pinned. If the rehearsal shows otherwise, the definer arms (the personal area's no-binning arm) fall back to the personal area's binding.
- Restating fn_edit_history_capture, which 14 tables share; a mistake silently drops history (EXCEPTION→WARNING).
  - *Mitigation:* A capture probe per entity type; post-conditions pin every 0061 arm; 17_edit_history green.
- The backfill walk mislabels rows if a capture was ever missed (WARNING path).
  - *Mitigation:* It can only narrow: an unresolvable or ambiguous row gets NULL (admins). Record the before and after counts in the hand-off.
- The same-project guard refuses a client path that writes a cross-project or just-deleted reference (INFERRED: none found; runMigration remaps within the project).
  - *Mitigation:* Whole pgTAP and vitest sets; the wilson-dev count of existing cross-project references is recorded; the refusal reads in plain words; array orphans pass.
- The epoch changes topic names: a client and server out of step after a narrowing (old build on a new server) lose live sync for that project.
  - *Mitigation:* Intended: they are exactly the subscribers who must stop hearing it. The creator's old build degrades to refetch; projects that never narrowed keep the plain topic.
- fn_realtime_broadcast is restated by four areas (this one, moves, bodies, NAS).
  - *Mitigation:* One chain: 0109 first, each later migration restating the latest with an md5 pre-flight and updating suites 100 and 101; the moves area drops its own files split.
- realtime.broadcast_changes behaviour with NULL records in downgrades.
  - *Mitigation:* Downgrades use realtime.send with an explicit jsonb payload in broadcast_changes' shape (the 0101 precedent); only unchanged both-sides events use broadcast_changes.
- The channel behaviour probes are vacuous on CI.
  - *Mitigation:* Body-structure and project_topic_matches probes bite on CI; the rolled-back wilson-dev rehearsal is mandatory and recorded with its planted-control results.
- The join cache for role or membership narrowing remains (a demoted admin or deactivated member already joined).
  - *Mitigation:* OUTSTANDING with the settling measurement; bounded by token life; pre-existing and outside Moth.

#### Interfaces with the other areas

- **projects owner guard** (provides): public.fn_projects_owner_guard() RETURNS trigger, plpgsql DEFINER, search_path=public; trigger trg_projects_owner_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.projects FOR EACH ROW. Bound callers: COALESCE(current_setting('role',true),'') IN ('authenticated','anon'). Pins created_by and topic_epoch; the is_private change is creator or admin only (42501); a producer_id change to non-NULL needs can_manage_project_roster(OLD.id) (42501); topic_epoch +1 on narrowing for all callers. The personal area RESTATES it (latest 0107) to add: refuse any change of personal_owner_id, is_private or created_by when OLD.personal_owner_id IS NOT NULL (42501, admins included), and refuse a deleted_at change on a personal row. No second projects guard.
- **projects.topic_epoch and project topic helpers** (provides): projects.topic_epoch integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 (guard-written only). public.project_topic(uuid) RETURNS text (DEFINER, revoked from clients): 'rabbit:project:<id>' at epoch 0, else 'rabbit:project:<id>:<n>'. public.project_topic_matches(text) RETURNS boolean (INVOKER, GRANT authenticated). Every project-topic sender (including later Moth ones) uses project_topic(); clients join with the index row's topic_epoch.
- **project hop shape and policy scans** (provides): Every client write clause and SELECT on a project-scoped table: `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = <t>.project_id)` under caller RLS, matched by `(FROM|JOIN) \(?projects [a-z_]+` plus the anchor column. Tables without project_id declare an anchor in the suite 110 anchor map or fail S4. Moth tables also carry the owner-only personal arm (S6). Definer-only tables (no authenticated privilege) are exempt.
- **same-project reference guard** (provides): public.fn_same_project_refs_guard() with trg_<t>_same_project_refs BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE on tasks, assets, phases, milestones, shots, folders, files, task_dependencies and phase_dependencies; fixed map (R6); 23503 'That item belongs to another project or no longer exists.'; skipped when current_setting('wilson.checked_move',true)='on'. A new link column on a project-scoped table must join the map or be a composite FK (S5).
- **wilson.checked_move setting (moves area)** (needs): Transaction-local GUC 'wilson.checked_move'='on', set only inside the checked move (moth_move_items / fn_moth_apply_moves). The move must clear or re-target entity links into the destination project in the same statement. files.folder_id and folders.parent_id become the moves area's DEFERRABLE composite FKs (S5 then counts them as composite).
- **move operation name and contract (moves vs bodies)** (needs): One name for the checked move: the moves area has public.moth_move_items(uuid[] file_ids, uuid[] folder_ids, uuid dest_project, uuid dest_folder) RETURNS jsonb and moth_undo_move(uuid); the bodies area calls it move_files. Integrator picks one. This area's contract: a cross-project files UPDATE yields an edit_history row with project_id NULL and a topic split (DELETE shape to the old topic, INSERT shape to the new) from 0109's fn_realtime_broadcast; the moves area does not restate its own files split.
- **files column guards and trusted writers (bodies, moves, NAS)** (needs): bodies: fn_files_body_guard (INVOKER, current_user IN ('authenticated','anon')) refuses client changes of project_id, workspace_id, storage_provider, storage_path, thumbnail_url, is_financial, folder_id; trusted writers are the checked move and rekey_file_body. moves: fn_files_move_guard (project_id change only under wilson.checked_move). NAS: fn_files_network_share_guard (writer GUC wilson.network_share_writer). This area's files_update and files_delete hops (policies) and the same-project guard (BEFORE trigger) coexist with them; trigger names sort so the Legal and body refusals keep their words.
- **body provider values** (needs): storage_provider gains 'network_share' (NAS, own ALTER TYPE migration, used only after commit). No dependency here: fn_realtime_broadcast's files arm keeps the money test first, and NAS adds `IF current_setting('wilson.network_share_writer',true)='on' THEN RETURN NULL` and 'network_share_checks' in the project_id arm by restating 0109.
- **file_events vocabulary** (needs): Conflict to reconcile: moves adds 'refiled' (details {direction:'out'|'in'|'within'}); bodies adds 'moved_out'/'moved_in'. One CHECK list restated whole. This area needs only that file_events_select stays gated by can_read_project_topic(project_id) (S2 allow-list) and that each event row names only its own project.
- **personal-space classifier** (needs): projects.personal_owner_id uuid; projects_personal_shape_chk CHECK (personal_owner_id IS NULL OR (is_private AND created_by = personal_owner_id)); projects_personal_owner_uniq. Readers: moth_can_read_target(kind,id) and moth_can_write_target(kind,id) with an owner-only personal arm and no admin leg. P1 lands after 0107 to 0109 and restates fn_projects_owner_guard and projects_update (adding `AND (personal_owner_id IS NULL OR personal_owner_id = auth.uid())`). The listProjects exclusion of personal spaces is the personal area's.
- **workspace-channel suppression** (provides): fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast (0109): never carries a row of a private (hence personal) project. Downgrades to INSERT or DELETE shape when one side is hidden. On a projects visibility flip sends realtime.send({table:'projects', schema:'public', operation:'AUDIENCE', record:null, old_record:null}, 'AUDIENCE', 'rabbit:workspace:<ws>', true). The same AUDIENCE payload goes to a narrowed project's old project topic. Client: adapter op 'AUDIENCE'; realtimeMerge effect {type:'audience'}; RabbitProvider requestIndexRevalidation().
- **edit history capture and audience** (provides): edit_history.project_id is set for every entity type through edit_history_project_of(text, jsonb); NULL for cross-project changes, mismatched dependency endpoints and cascaded children; edit_history.captured_private boolean; edit_history_select adds the R8 arm. Independent of moth_history (tables area), which stays definer-only with no client grants (S2 exempts it) and never reads topic_epoch.
- **money gate** (provides): can_access_project_money(uuid) now includes passes_project_privacy(p_project). Every money and Legal consumer in every area inherits it; the client mirror canSeeProjectMoney is unchanged (it runs only for visible projects).
- **NAS network_share_checks** (needs): If authenticated can read it, its SELECT policy carries the project hop (S2); it joins fn_realtime_broadcast's project_id arm by restating 0109, which sends to project_topic(project_id).
